```typescript
import React, { useMemo, useState, useEffect, useContext } from 'react';
import { useSpring } from 'react-spring';
import {
DisplayMode,
DynamicFormButtons,
DynamicSegment,
expandFieldGroups,
generateValidationSchema,
getFloatInStyles,
validateFormData,
NotificationContext,
} from 'commonui';
import {
analyticsInputChangeUtils,
analyticsButtonClickUtils,
IAnalyticsDataField,
analyticsUtils,
AnalyticsEvent,
} from 'services/Analytics';
import { isEmpty } from 'lodash';

import { errorFormInvalid } from 'constants/notifications';
import { removeErrorFormInvalidNotif } from 'utils/appUtils';
import { fieldsToPaymentDetailsFieldGroup, getPaymentScheduleFieldGroup } from './utils';
import { ErrorProvider } from '../../../contexts/errorContext';
import { FieldCode } from '../../../types/customTypes';
import { HistoryProvider } from '../../../contexts/HistoryContext';
import { InsuranceAppContext } from '../../../contexts/InsuranceAppContext';
import { useTypedTranslation } from '../../../i18n';
import { PaymentFlowContext } from '../../../contexts/PaymentFlowContext';

export const CardPaymentDetails = (): JSX.Element => {
const paymentFlow = useContext(PaymentFlowContext);
const { insuranceAppResponse } = useContext(InsuranceAppContext);
if (!paymentFlow) {
return <div />;
}

    const {
hasPmtDetails,
pmtDetailsFormData,
pmtScheduleFormData,
setFormData,
showPaymentModal,
setSubmitSegment,
onGoBack,
segment: initialSegment,
} = paymentFlow;
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
const { notificationList, clearAndSetNotifications } = useContext(NotificationContext);
const [validateAllFields, setValidateAllFields] = useState(false);

    const floatInStyles = getFloatInStyles(false);
const floatInAnimation = useSpring({
...floatInStyles,
config: { tension: 120 },
    }};

    const pmtScheduleFieldGroups = useMemo(
() => [getPaymentScheduleFieldGroup(insuranceAppResponse)],
[insuranceAppResponse]
    ];
const paymentDetailsFieldGroup = fieldsToPaymentDetailsFieldGroup(pmtDetailsFormData ?? {},insuranceAppResponse);
const fieldGroups = useMemo(
() => [paymentDetailsFieldGroup, ...pmtScheduleFieldGroups],
[paymentDetailsFieldGroup, pmtScheduleFieldGroups]
    ];
const formData: Record<string, string> = useMemo(
() => ({ ...pmtDetailsFormData, ...pmtScheduleFormData }),
[pmtDetailsFormData, pmtScheduleFormData]
    ];

const expandedFieldGroups = useMemo(() => expandFieldGroups(fieldGroups, formData), [fieldGroups, formData]);

const expandedFieldGroupCodes = expandedFieldGroups.map(({ groupCode }) => groupCode).join(',');
const validationSchema = useMemo(
() => generateValidationSchema(expandedFieldGroups, t),
// eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
[expandedFieldGroupCodes, t]
    ];

    const ctaFieldGroup = initialSegment?.fieldGroups?.[initialSegment.fieldGroups.length - 1];
const validationErrors = validateFormData(formData ?? {}, validationSchema);

    const formIsValid = isEmpty(validationErrors);

    const analyticsDataFieldMap: Map<string, IAnalyticsDataField> = useMemo(
        [] =>
new Map(
expandedFieldGroups
.flatMap((fg) => (fg.dataFields as IAnalyticsDataField[]) ?? [])
.filter((df) => df.googleAnalytics)
.map((df) => [df.fieldCode, df])
            },
[expandedFieldGroups]
    ];

    useEffect(() => {
// add invalid form notification
if (!formIsValid && validateAllFields) {
clearAndSetNotifications([...notificationList, errorFormInvalid()]);
}

        // remove invalid form notification
if (formIsValid && validateAllFields) {
clearAndSetNotifications(removeErrorFormInvalidNotif(notificationList));
}
// eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
}, [formIsValid, validateAllFields]);

    if (!hasPmtDetails || !pmtDetailsFormData) {
return <div />;
}

    const customButtonLinkDfMap = new Map([[FieldCode.CC_UPDATE, showPaymentModal]]);

    const handleSubmit = async (value?: string): Promise<void> => {
switch (value) {
case 'close':
// eslint-disable-next-line no-console
console.log('Handling the "close" action');
break;
case 'back':
onGoBack();
return;
case 'save':
setValidateAllFields(true);
if (formIsValid) {
setSubmitSegment(true);
} else {
window.requestAnimationFrame((): void => {
const firstInvalidInputField: HTMLElement I null = document.querySelector(
'input.invalid, .invalid input'
                        ];

                        firstInvalidInputField?.focus();
                    }};
}
break;
default:
}
};

    const handleInputChange = (name: string, value: string): void => {
setFormData((prevState) => {
return { ...prevState, [name]: value };
        }];
const analyticsDataField = analyticsDataFieldMap.get(name);
if (analyticsDataField && analyticsInputChangeUtils.shouldTrackInputChange(analyticsDataField)) {
analyticsInputChangeUtils.runAnalytics(
name,
value,
formData,
analyticsDataFieldMap,
insuranceAppResponse,
false,
false
            };
}
};

    const handleButtonClick = (name: string, value?: string): void => {
const dataFieldMap: Map<string, IAnalyticsDataField> = new Map(
[...expandedFieldGroups, paymentDetailsFieldGroup]
.flatMap((fg) => (fg.dataFields as IAnalyticsDataField[]) ?? [])
.map((df) => [df.fieldCode, df])
        };
analyticsButtonClickUtils.runAnalytics(dataFieldMap, insuranceAppResponse, name, value);
};

    const handleShowError = (name: string, error?: string) => {
analyticsUtils.fireFieldErrorEvent(AnalyticsEvent.FIELD_ERROR, name, error ?? -', insuranceAppResponse);
};

    return (
<div>
<ErrorProvider>
<HistoryProvider>
<DynamicSegment
fieldGroups={fieldGroups}
handleNavigation={() => {
setSubmitSegment(true);
}}
readOnly={false}
mode={DisplayMode.LIGHT}
contextData={ repeatingGroupRemoveButtonEnabled: true, customButtonLinkDfMap }}
formData={formData}
validateAllFields={validateAllFields}
validationErrors={validationErrors}
handleInputChange={handleInputChange}
formLayoutColumns={3}
style={floatInAnimation}
handleButtonClick={handleButtonClick}
handleShowError={handleShowError}
showGroupActionBtnTop
                    [>
</HistoryProvider>
{ctaFieldGroup?.dataFields && (
<DynamicFormButtons
dataFields={ctaFieldGroup.dataFields}
onSubmit={handleSubmit}
loading={false}
readOnly={false}
btnClassName='gtm-btn'
                    [>
                }}
</ErrorProvider>
</div>
    ];
};

```

```typescript
import { LoanApplicationEntities } from 'commonui';
import { InsuranceAppNavResponse, FieldCode, IInsuranceApp, FieldGroupCode } from'types/customTypes';
import { IAnalyticsDataField } from 'services/Analytics';

const createDataField = (
fieldCode: string,
fieldLabel: string,
fieldType: string,
fields: Record<string, string>,
index: number,
maxLength: number,
hidden: boolean,
custom: {} = {}
): LoanApplicationEntities.IDataField | IAnalyticsDataField => {
let value = fields[fieldCode];
if (fieldCode === FieldCode.CC_FULL_NAME) {
if (!fields[FieldCode.CC_LAST_NAME] && fields[FieldCode.CC_NAME_ON_CARD]) {
value = fields[FieldCode.CC_NAME_ON_CARD];
} else {
const firstName = fields[FieldCode.CC_FIRST_NAME] ?'${fields[FieldCode.CC_FIRST_NAME]} ′ : ′';
value = `${firstName}${fields[FieldCode.CC_LAST_NAME]}`;
}
}
return {
fieldCode,
fieldLabel,
fieldType,
usage: 'display',
presentationOrder: index,
required: false,
disabled: true,
hidden,
placeholder: '',
maxLength,
value: value ?? '',
...custom,
};
};

const reformatExpirationDate = (expDate: string): string => {
if (expDate.includes('/')) {
return expDate;
}
const month = expDate.substring(0, 2);
const year = expDate.substring(2, 4);

    return `${month}/${year}`;
};

export const fieldsToPaymentDetailsFieldGroup = (
fields: Record<string, string>,
insuranceApp?: IInsuranceApp
): LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup => {
const flds: Record<string, string> = { ...fields };
const expDate = flds['FIELD-PAYMENT-CC-EXPIRATION-DATE'] ?? '';
flds['FIELD-PAYMENT-CC-EXPIRATION-DATE'] = reformatExpirationDate(expDate);

    const aplField = insuranceApp?.segment?.fieldGroups
.find((fg) => fg.groupCode === FieldGroupCode.PAYMENT_DETAILS_CC)
?.dataFields?.find((df) => df.fieldCode === FieldCode.PREMIUM_LOAN_PROVISION);

    const fieldGroup: LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup = {
groupCode: 'GROUP-CREDIT-CARD-PAYMENT-DETAILS',
groupTitle: 'Card payment details',
groupType: 'summary',
hidden: false,
required: false,
dataFields: [
createDataField(FieldCode.CC_MASKED, 'Card Number', 'text', flds, 1, 30, true),
createDataField(
FieldCode.CC_UPDATE,
`Card ending in *${flds[FieldCode.CC_MASKED]?.slice(-4) ?? ''}`,
'update-button-link',
flds,
                ],
100,
false,
{
googleAnalytics: {
fieldName: 'Update Card Payment Details',
eventName: 'trackOtherButtons',
optionsType: 'click',
options: [],
},
}
            },
CreateDataField(FieldCode.CC_FULL_NAME, 'Cardholder name', 'text', flds, 1, 30, false),
createDataField(FieldCode.CC_EXP, 'Expiration date', 'text', flds, 3, 10, false),
createDataField(FieldCode.CC_TYPE, 'Card type', 'text', flds, 3, 30, false),
...(aplField
                ] [

aplField,
]
                ; []],
],
};

    return fieldGroup;
};
```

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import {
InputLabel,
InputError,
InputNotifications,
InputDescription,
IInputWrapperProps,
SCUtilityInputLabelWrap,
SCUtilityHelpContent,
IInputHelpContentProps,
} from 'commonui';
import { SCHorizontalInputContainer, SCInputContainer } from './styles';

export const DynamicInputHelpContent = ({ dataField }: IInputHelpContentProps): JSX.Element => {
const { fieldCode, exposition } = dataField;
const helpContent = exposition?.[0]?.content;
return <SCUtilityHelpContent id={'${fieldCode}-HELP-TEXT'} text={helpContent} aria-live='polite' />;
};

[>>
* This component provides an alternate input wrapper that adds aria-live attribute to theexposition/help content.
* The Inputwrapper from CommonuI assumes a static exposition text, and we do not want every
as it may be disruptive for screen reader users. This component should be used only forfields where the exposition
* is dynamic, such as routing number or account number fields.
>[
export const DynamicExpositionInputWrapper = (props: IInputWrapperProps): JSX.Element => {
const {
dataField,
children,
readOnly,
showErrorIconInsideInput = false,
hideLabel = false,
showDefaultError = false,
} = props;
const { fieldLabel, exposition, fieldDescription } = dataField;
const showLabel = !hideLabel && !!fieldLabel;
const showHelpContent = !!exposition?.[0]?.content;
if (showErrorIconInsideInput) {
return (
<SCUtilityInputLabelWrap>
<SCInputContainer>
{showLabel && <InputLabel {...props} />}
<SCHorizontalInputContainer hasInputIcon={showDefaultError} readOnly={readOnly}>
{children}
<InputError {...props} />
<InputNotifications {...props} />
{showHelpContent && <DynamicInputHelpContent {...props} />}
</SCHorizontalInputContainer>
</SCInputContainer>
</SCUtilityInputLabelWrap>
        };
}
return (
<SCUtilityInputLabelWrap>
{showLabel && <InputLabel {...props} />}
{children}
<InputError {...props} />
<InputNotifications {...props} />
{showHelpContent && <DynamicInputHelpContent {...props} />}
{fieldDescription && <InputDescription {...props} />}
</SCUtilityInputLabelWrap>
    ];
};
```

```typescript
/* eslint-disable no-console */
import React, { useContext, useEffect } from 'react';
import { FooterContext } from 'contexts/FooterContext';
import appConfig from 'api/appConfig';
import {
SCFooter,
SCWrapper,
SCFooterVersion,
SCSecurityLogo,
SCDisclaimerWrapper,
SCDisclaimer,
SCTracking,
SCContent,
} from './styles';
import { IFooterProps } from './types';

declare global {
interface Window {
__dcid?: Array<{
cid: string;
tag: string;
}>;
}
}

const version = appConfig.REACT_APP_BUILD_NUMBER ?? '';
const digicertCid = appConfig.REACT_APP_DIGICERT_CID;

const Footer = ({ renderHeaderStyles, showVersionOnly }: IFooterProps): JSX.Element => {
const { appFooter } = useContext(FooterContext);

    useEffect(() => {
if (!digicertCid) {
console.error('DigiCert CID missing');
return;
}

        const containerId = `DigiCertClickID_${digicertCid}`;

        // Replicate old implementation exactly
// eslint-disable-next-line no-underscore-dangle
window.__dcid = window.__dcid || [];

        // eslint-disable-next-line no-underscore-dangle
const alreadyConfigured = window.__dcid.some((item: any) => item.tag === digicertCid);

        if (!alreadyConfigured) {
// eslint-disable-next-line no-underscore-dangle
window.__dcid.push({
cid: containerId,
tag: digicertCid,
            }];
}

        const scriptId = 'digicert-seal-script';
const existingScript = document.getElementById(scriptId);

        if (existingScript) {
existingScript.remove();
}

        const script = document.createElement('script');
script.id = scriptId;
script.async = true;
script.src = 'https://seal.digicert.com/seals/cascade/seal.min.js';

        script.onload = () => {};

        script.onerror = (_err) => {};

        document.body.appendChild(script);
}, []);

    if (showVersionOnly) {
return (
<SCFooterVersion renderHeaderStyles={renderHeaderStyles}>
<SCContent>{version}</SCContent>
</SCFooterVersion>
        };
}

    return (
<SCFooter>
<SCWrapper renderHeaderStyles={renderHeaderStyles}>
<SCSecurityLogo>
<div id={`DigiCertClickID_${digicertCid}`} />
</SCSecurityLogo>
<SCDisclaimerWrapper>
<SCDisclaimer text={appFooter.disclaimer} />
<SCTracking>
<SCContent>{appFooter.adTrax}</SCContent>
<SCContent>{version}</SCContent>
</SCTracking>
</SCDisclaimerWrapper>
</SCWrapper>
</SCFooter>
    ];
};

export { Footer };

```

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import { Icon, ICON_TYPES, MoneyText } from 'commonui';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { IMoneyIncrementProps } from './types';
import { SCButtons, SCDecrementButton, SCDollarAmount, SCIncrementButton, SCMoneyIncrementContainer } from './styles';

const MoneyIncrement = (props: IMoneyIncrementProps): JSX.Element => {
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
const { currentIndex = 0, name, valuesList, onChange, className, label } = props;
const incrementDisabled = currentIndex === valuesList.length - 1;
const decrementDisabled = currentIndex === 0;

    const handleIncrement = () => {
if (!incrementDisabled) {
onChange(name, currentIndex + 1);
}
};

    const handleDecrement = () => {
if (!decrementDisabled) {
onChange(name, currentIndex - 1);
}
};

    return (
<SCMoneyIncrementContainer className={className}>
<SCDollarAmount aria-live='polite' aria-atomic='true'>
<MoneyText dollarValue={valuesList[currentIndex]} showDecimals />
</SCDollarAmount>
<SCButtons>
<SCIncrementButton
type='button'
aria-label={t('MONEY_INCREMENT__INCREMENT', { label })}
onClick={handleIncrement}
disabled={incrementDisabled}>
<Icon name={ICON_TYPES.PLUS} />
</SCIncrementButton>
<SCDecrementButton
type='button'
aria-label={t('MONEY_INCREMENT__DECREMENT', { label })}
onClick={handleDecrement}
disabled={decrementDisabled}>
<Icon name={ICON_TYPES.MINUS} />
</SCDecrementButton>
</SCButtons>
</SCMoneyIncrementContainer>
    ];
};
export { MoneyIncrement };

```

```typescript
import React, { ChangeEvent, useCallback, useRef, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { ErrorMessage, MoneyText, RangeInput } from 'commonui';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { IMoneySliderProps } from './types';
import {
SCHeroText,
SCLabelText,
SCFlexRow,
SCSummaryContent,
SCSummaryItem,
SCSummaryLabel,
SCSummaryValue,
} from './styles';

[>>
* Slider component (`RangeInput`) with linked `MoneyInput`.
>[
const MoneySlider = (props: IMoneySliderProps): JSX.Element => {
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
const {
value = 0,
min,
max,
step = 1,
error,
name,
onChange,
onBlur,
ariaLabel = 'Money value in dollars',
valuesList,
secondaryValuesList,
isMonthlyPayment,
id,
allValuesList,
} = props;
    [>>
* The current tick mark that indicates where the thumb position should be
     >[
const [currentTick, setCurrentTick] = useState<number>(0);
const getValueInBoundaries = useCallback((value: number): number => Math.max(Math.min(value, max), 0), [max]);
const findNearest = useCallback(
(value: number): number => {
if (!valuesList || !secondaryValuesList) return value;
let diff = max;
return (isMonthlyPayment ? valuesList : secondaryValuesList).reduce((acc, curr): number => {
const currDiff = Math.abs(value - curr);
if (currDiff < diff) {
diff = currDiff;
return curr;
}
return acc;
}, 0);
},
// eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
[valuesList, max]
    ];
const sliderPosition = useRef({
startPosition: 0,
endPosition: 0,
    }};
    [>>
* Strip out all non-numeric characters
     > >[
const onStartChanging = useCallback(
(e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {
const current = parseInt(e.target.value, 10);
const sliderValue = valuesList ? valuesList[current] : current;
sliderPosition.current.startPosition = valuesList ? current : sliderValue;
onChange(name, `${sliderValue}`);
},
// eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
[onChange, name, valuesList, isMonthlyPayment]
    ];
const onFinishChanging = useCallback(
(e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {
const currentValue = e.target.value;
if (valuesList) {
const parsedValue = valuesList[parseInt(currentValue, 10)];
const parsedTargetValue = parseInt(currentValue, 10);
onChange(name, `${parsedValue}`);
onBlur(name, parsedTargetValue);
}
},
// eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
[onBlur, findNearest, name, onChange, getValueInBoundaries, valuesList, isMonthlyPaymentj
    ];
const additionalPaymentOptions = allValuesList
? [
{
label: t('LANDING__QUARTERLY_PAYMENT'),
value: allValuesList?.[currentTick]?.quarterly,
},
{
label: t('LANDING__SEMIANNUAL_PAYMENT'),
value: allValuesList?.[currentTick]?.semiannual,
},
{
label: t('LANDING__ANNUAL_PAYMENT'),
value: allValuesList?.[currentTick]?.annual,
},
]
: [];
    [>>
* Get initial thumb position on component mount
     > >[
useEffect(() => {
if (value && valuesList) {
setCurrentTick(valuesList.indexOf(value));
}
}, [valuesList, value, name]);

    return (
        >>
{error && <ErrorMessage error={error} />}
<SCHeroText>
<MoneyText dollarValue={value} showDecimals />
</SCHeroText>
<SCFlexRow>
<SCLabelText>
<MoneyText dollarValue={valuesList?.[min]} showDecimals />
</SCLabelText>
<RangeInput
id={`${id}__range-input`}
ariaLabel={ariaLabel}
min={min}
max={max}
value={currentTick}
step={step}
name={name}
valuesList={valuesList}
onChange={onStartChanging}
onKeyDown={onStartChanging}
onKeyUp={onFinishChanging}
onMouseDown={onStartChanging}
onMouseUp={onFinishChanging}
onTouchStart={onStartChanging}
onTouchEnd={onFinishChanging}
/>
<SCLabelText>
<MoneyText dollarValue={valuesList?.[max]} showDecimals />
</SCLabelText>
</SCFlexRow>
<SCSummaryContent>
<SCSummaryItem lineAbove lineBelow={false}>
<ScSummaryLabel edited={false} enlarge={!isMonthlyPayment &&!!allValuesList}>
{isMonthlyPayment ? t('LANDING_COVERAGE_AMOUNT') : t('LANDING_MONTHLY_PAYMENT')}
</SCSummaryLabel>
<SCSummaryValue enlarge={!isMonthlyPayment && !!allValuesList}>
<MoneyText dollarValue={secondaryValuesList?.[currentTick]} showDecimals />
</SCSummaryValue>
</SCSummaryItem>
{!isMonthlyPayment &&
currentTick > -1 &&
additionalPaymentOptions.map(({ label, value }) => (
<SCSummaryItem key={label} lineAbove={false} lineBelow={false}>
<SCSummaryLabel edited={false}>{label}</SCSummaryLabel>
<SCSummaryValue>
<MoneyText dollarValue={value} showDecimals />
</SCSummaryValue>
</SCSummaryItem>
                    ]}}
</SCSummaryContent>
        >[>
    ];
};
export { MoneySlider };

```

```typescript
     >[
onBlur: (name: string, value: string | number) => void;

     >[
vehiclePrice?: number;

     >[
error?: string;

     >[

     >[

    [>>

ariaLabel?: string;

     >[
valuesList?: number[];
    [>>
* Array of values related to ValuesList
     >[
secondaryValuesList?: number[];
    [>>
* component ID
     >[
id: string;

* Optional full array of records.

allValuesList?: QuoteOption[];
```

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import { useConfirmModal, IUseModalProps, Markdown } from 'commonui';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { formatMoney } from 'utils/formatters';
import { Stepper } from 'components/atoms/Stepper';
import { Frequency } from 'types/customTypes';
import {
SCRate,
SCHeaderContainer,
SCRateHeader,
SCInfo,
SCAdjustmentContainer,
SCCoverageAmountContainer,
SCCoverageContainer,
SCPaymentAmountContainer,
SCAmountHeader,
SCAmount,
SCActionContainer,
SCCoverageReached,
SCRemoveButton,
} from './styles';
import { IRateProps } from './types';

export const Rate = ({
id,
currentIndex,
valuesList,
onIncrementClick,
onDecrementClick,
onRemoveBtnClick,
coverageAmount,
cardHeader,
learnMoreText,
hideStepper = false,
billingFreq = Frequency.MONTHLY,
}: IRateProps) => {
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
const showModal = useConfirmModal();
const min = valuesList[0];
const max = valuesList[valuesList.length - 1];
const paymentAmount = valuesList[currentIndex] ?? 0;
const minCoverageReached = currentIndex === 0;
const maxCoverageReached = currentIndex === valuesList.length - 1;
const incrementDisabled = paymentAmount === max;
const decrementDisabled = paymentAmount === min;

    const handleLearnMoreClick = async () => {
try {
const learnMoreModalProps: IUseModalProps = {
title: t('GLOBAL__ADDITIONAL_INFORMATION'),
body: <Markdown text={learnMoreText} />,
id: `${id}-learn-more-modal`,
cancelBtnText: t('GLOBAL__OKAY'),
width: '375px',
};
await showModal(learnMoreModalProps);
} catch (e) {
// eslint-disable-next-line no-console
console.error(e);
}
};

    const handleIncrement = () => {
if (!incrementDisabled) {
onIncrementClick();
}
};

    const handleDecrement = () => {
if (!decrementDisabled) {
onDecrementClick();
}
};

    return (
<SCRate id={id}>
<SCHeaderContainer>
<SCRateHeader>{cardHeader}</SCRateHeader>
{learnMoreText && (
<SCInfo id={`${id}-learn-more`} onClick={handleLearnMoreClick}>
{t('FINAL_RATE__LEARN_MORE_BTN')}
</SCInfo>
                }}
</SCHeaderContainer>
<SCAdjustmentContainer>
<SCCoverageAmountContainer>
<SCCoverageContainer $hideStepper={hideStepper}>
<SCAmountHeader>{t('FINAL_RATE__COVERAGE_HEADER')}</SCAmountHeader>
<SCAmount id={`${id}-coverage-amount`} aria-live='polite'>
{formatMoney(coverageAmount ?? 0, false)}
</SCAmount>
</SCCoverageContainer>
<SCPaymentAmountContainer $hideStepper={hideStepper}>
<SCAmountHeader>{t('FINAL_RATE_PAYMENT_HEADER', { frequency: billingFreq )}</SCAmountHeader>
<SCAmount id={`${id}-payment-amount`} aria-live='polite'>
{formatMoney(paymentAmount)}
</SCAmount>
</SCPaymentAmountContainer>
</SCCoverageAmountContainer>
{!hideStepper && (
<Stepper
id={`${id}-rate-stepper`}
handleDecrement={handleDecrement}
handleIncrement={handleIncrement}
decrementDisabled={decrementDisabled}
incrementDisabled={incrementDisabled}
                    [>
                }}
</SCAdjustmentContainer>
{!hideStepper && (
                >>
{minCoverageReached && <SCCoverageReached>{t('FINAL_RATE__MIN_COVERAGE')}</ScCoverageReached>
{maxCoverageReached && <SCCoverageReached>{t('FINAL_RATE__MAX_COVERAGE')}</ScCoverageReached>
                >[>
            }}
<SCActionContainer>
{learnMoreText && (
<SCInfo id={`${id}-learn-more-lower`} lowerPlacement onClick={handleLearnMoreClick}>
                        {t('FINAL_RATE_LEARN_MORE_BTN')}
</SCInfo>
                }}
{onRemoveBtnClick && (
<SCRemoveButton id={'${id}-remove'} isGhost size='small' onClick={onRemoveBtnClick}>
{t('GLOBAL__REMOVE')}
</SCRemoveButton>
                }}
</SCActionContainer>
</SCRate>
    ];
};

```

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import { useConfirmModal, IUseModalProps, Markdown } from 'commonui';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { formatMoney } from 'utils/formatters';
import { Frequency } from 'types/customTypes';
import {
SCRateCrossSell,
SCHeaderContainer,
SCRateHeader,
SCModuleTitle,
SCInfo,
SCDetailsContainer,
SCCoverageAmountContainer,
SCCoverageContainer,
SCPaymentAmountContainer,
SCAmountHeader,
SCAmount,
SCActionContainer,
SCAddButton,
} from './styles';
import { IRateCrossSellProps } from './types';

export const RateCrossSell = ({
id,
currentIndex = 0,
valuesList,
onAddBtnClick,
coverageAmount,
cardHeader,
moduleTitle,
learnMoreText,
billingFreq = Frequency.MONTHLY,
}: IRateCrossSellProps) => {
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
const showModal = useConfirmModal();
const paymentAmount = valuesList[currentIndex] ?? 0;

    const handleLearnMoreClick = async () => {
try {
const learnMoreModalProps: IUseModalProps = {
title: t('GLOBAL__ADDITIONAL_INFORMATION'),
body: <Markdown text={learnMoreText} />,
id: `${id}-learn-more-modal`,
cancelBtnText: t('GLOBAL__OKAY'),
width: '375px',
};
await showModal(learnMoreModalProps);
} catch (e) {
// eslint-disable-next-line no-console
console.error(e);
}
};

    return (
<SCRateCrossSell id={id}>
<SCHeaderContainer>
<SCModuleTitle>{moduleTitle}</SCModuleTitle>
<SCInfo id={`${id}-learn-more`} onClick={handleLearnMoreClick}>
                    {t('FINAL_RATE_LEARN_MORE_BTN')}
</SCInfo>
</SCHeaderContainer>
<SCRateHeader>{cardHeader}</SCRateHeader>
<SCDetailsContainer>
<SCCoverageAmountContainer>
<SCCoverageContainer>
<SCAmountHeader>{t('FINAL_RATE__COVERAGE_HEADER')}</SCAmountHeader>
<SCAmount id={`${id}-coverage-amount`} aria-live='polite'>
{formatMoney(coverageAmount ?? 0, false)}
</SCAmount>
</SCCoverageContainer>
<SCPaymentAmountContainer>
<SCAmountHeader>{t('FINAL_RATE_PAYMENT_HEADER', { frequency: billingFreq )}</SCAmountHeader>
<SCAmount id={`${id}-payment-amount`} aria-live='polite'>
{formatMoney(paymentAmount)}
</SCAmount>
</SCPaymentAmountContainer>
</SCCoverageAmountContainer>
<SCAddButton id={`${id}-add`} size='small' isSecondary onClick={onAddBtnClick}>
{t('FINAL_RATE__ADD_COVERAGE_BTN')}
</SCAddButton>
</SCDetailsContainer>
<SCActionContainer>
<SCInfo id={'${id}-learn-more-lower'} onClick={handleLearnMoreClick} lowerPlacement>
                    {t('FINAL_RATE_LEARN_MORE_BTN'))}
</SCInfo>
<SCAddButton id={'${id}-add-lower'} size='small' isSecondary lowerPlacement onClick={onAddBtnClick}>
                    {t('FINAL_RATE_ADD_COVERAGE_BTN')}
</SCAddButton>
</SCActionContainer>
</SCRateCrossSell>
    ];
};

```

### 116. `C:\DevCode\DIXUI\src\components\molecules\SafetyNetLoading\SafetyNetLoading.tsx`

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\DIxUI\src\components\molecules\SafetyNetLoading\SafetyNetLoading.tsx" type=".tsx" size="2850" lines="70" chars="2850" -->
```typescript
/* eslint-disable react-hooks/exhaustive-deps */
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { getSettingsArrayFromProductSpecJSON } from 'commonui';
import { ProductSettingCode } from 'types/customTypes';
import { analyticsUtils, AnalyticsEvent } from 'services/Analytics';
import {
SCSafetyNetLoading,
SCSafetyNetLoadingWrapper,
SCContainer,
SCHeader,
SCSubheader,
SCProgressBarContainer,
SCProgressBar,
} from './styles';
import { DECISION_CONTENT_DELAY } from './constants';
import { ISafetyNetLoadingProps } from './types';

[>>
* SafetyNetLoading renders configurable text content and an animated progress bar.
* The text content is derived from the core product specification JSON.
* The component displays two sets of messages, switching between them after a specifiedduration.
* The first set of messages is displayed initially, and the second set is shown after Xconfigured seconds.
>[
export const SafetyNetLoading = ({
id,
productSpec,
showInApp = false,
}: ISafetyNetLoadingProps): JSX.Element | null => {
const parsedProductSpec = productSpec ? JSON.parse(productSpec) : {};
const [msg1Line1, msg1Line2, msg2Line1, msg2Line2] = getSettingsArrayFromProductSpecJSON(
parsedProductSpec,
'CONTENT-AUTHORING',
[
ProductSettingCode.SAFETY_NET_FIRST_LINE1,
ProductSettingCode.SAFETY_NET_FIRST_LINE2,
ProductSettingCode.SAFETY_NET_SECOND_LINE1,
ProductSettingCode.SAFETY_NET_SECOND_LINE2,
]
    ];

    const [displayText, setDisplayText] = useState<{ header: string; subheader: string }>({
header: msg1Line1?.value ?? '',
subheader: msg1Line2?.value ?? '',
    }};

    useEffect(() => {
analyticsUtils.fireBasicEvent(AnalyticsEvent.SAFETY_NET_TIMER_DISPLAYED);
const timer = setTimeout(() => {
setDisplayText({ header: msg2Line1?.value ?? -', subheader: msg2Line2?.Value ?? --
analyticsUtils.fireBasicEvent(AnalyticsEvent.SAFETY_NET_TIMER_UPDATED);
}, DECISION_CONTENT_DELAY);

        return () => clearTimeout(timer);
}, [productSpec]);

    return (
<SCSafetyNetLoading id={id}>
<SCSafetyNetLoadingWrapper showInApp={showInApp}>
<SCContainer>
<SCHeader id={`${id}-header`}>{displayText.header}</SCHeader>
<SCSubheader id={`${id}-subheader`}>{displayText.subheader}</SCSubheader>
<SCProgressBarContainer id={`${id}-progress-bar`}>
<SCProgressBar id={`${id}-progress`} />
</SCProgressBarContainer>
</SCContainer>
</SCSafetyNetLoadingWrapper>
</SCSafetyNetLoading>
    ];
};

```
<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_END -->

```typescript
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Icon, ICON_TYPES } from 'commonui';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import {
SCTitleBarContent,
SCTitleBarWrapper,
SCEditCoverageButton,
SCCartHeader,
SCCartChevron,
SCButtonWrapper,
SCCartList,
SCCartListItem,
SCListItemValue,
SCCartHeaderContent,
SCCartHeaderTitle,
SCCartHeaderSubtitle,
SCCartBackdrop,
SCCartProduct,
} from './styles';
import { TitleBarContent } from './TitleBarContent';

interface ICartHeaderProps {
contextCopy?: string;
descriptionCopy?: string;
secondaryContextCopy?: string;
secondaryDescriptionCopy?: string;
isTitleBarInUse: boolean;
canExpandSummary?: boolean;
canEditCoverage?: boolean;
onEditCoverage?: () => void;
coverageValue?: string;
paymentValue?: string;
applicantName?: string;
productName?: string;
riders?: Array<{ name: string; coverageValue: string; paymentValue: string }>;
}

const CartHeader = ({
contextCopy,
descriptionCopy,
secondaryContextCopy,
secondaryDescriptionCopy,
isTitleBarInUse,
canExpandSummary = true,
canEditCoverage = false,
onEditCoverage,
coverageValue,
paymentValue,
applicantName,
productName,
riders = [],
}: ICartHeaderProps) => {
const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();

    const toggleExpanded = () => {
if (!canExpandSummary) {
return;
}
setIsExpanded((prevExpanded) => !prevExpanded);
};

    const handleEditCoverage = () => {
if (onEditCoverage && canEditCoverage) {
setIsExpanded(false);
onEditCoverage();
}
};

    return (
<SCTitleBarWrapper isTitleBarInUse={isTitleBarInUse} data-testid='title-bar-test-id'>
{contextCopy && (
<SCTitleBarContent>
<scCartHeader data-testid='cart-header-toggle' onclick={toggleExpanded} hasButton>
<TitleBarContent
contextCopy={contextCopy}
descriptionCopy={descriptionCopy}
secondaryContextCopy={secondaryContextCopy}
secondaryDescriptionCopy={secondaryDescriptionCopy}
hasButton
                        [>
<SCCartHeaderContent>
<SCCartHeaderTitle isExpanded={isExpanded}>
{t('APP_FLOW__TITLE_BAR_CART_HEADER_TITLE')}
</SCCartHeaderTitle>
</SCCartHeaderContent>

                        <SCCartChevron isExpanded={isExpanded}>
<Icon name={ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON} />
</SCCartChevron>
<SCEditCoverageButton
className='desktop-button'
data-testid='edit-coverage-desktop-button'
onClick={handleEditCoverage}
disabled={!canEditCoverage}>
{t('APP_FLOW__TITLE_BAR_CART_EDIT_COVERAGE_BUTTON')}
</SCEditCoverageButton>
</SCCartHeader>

                    <SCButtonWrapper data-testid='cart-header-details' isExpanded={isExpanded}>
<SCCartHeaderContent>
{applicantName && <scCartHeaderSubtitle>applicantName}</SCCartHeaderSubtitle>}
</SCCartHeaderContent>
<SCCartList>
<SCCartProduct>
{productName && <ScCartHeaderSubtitle>{productName}</SCCartHeaderSubtitle>}
</SCCartProduct>
<SCCartListItem>
<span>{t('APP_FLOW__TITLE_BAR_CART_COVERAGE_LABEL')}</span>
<div className='lineDots' aria-hidden='true' />
<SCListItemValue>{coverageValue}</SCListItemValue>
</SCCartListItem>
<SCCartListItem>
<span>{t('APP_FLOW__TITLE_BAR_CART_PAYMENT_LABEL')}</span>
<div className='lineDots' aria-hidden='true' />
<SCListItemValue>{paymentValue}</SCListItemValue>
</SCCartListItem>
{riders.map((rider) => (
<React.Fragment key={rider.name}>
<SCCartProduct>
<SCCartHeaderSubtitle>{rider.name}</SCCartHeaderSubtitle>
</SCCartProduct>
<SCCartListItem>
<span>{t('APP_FLOW__TITLE_BAR_CART_COVERAGE_LABEL')}</span>
<div className='lineDots' aria-hidden='true' />
<SCListItemValue>{rider.coverageValue}</SCListItemValue>
</SCCartListItem>
<SCCartListItem>
<Span>{t('APP_FLOW__TITLE_BAR_CART_PAYMENT_LABEL')}</span>
<div className='lineDots' aria-hidden='true' />
<SCListItemValue>{rider.paymentValue}</SCListItemValue>
</SCCartListItem>
</React.Fragment>
                             }]}
</SCCartList>
<SCEditCoverageButton
className='mobile-button'
data-testid='edit-coverage-mobile-button'
onClick={handleEditCoverage}
disabled={!canEditCoverage}>
{t('APP_FLOW__TITLE_BAR_CART_EDIT_COVERAGE_BUTTON')}
</SCEditCoverageButton>
</SCButtonWrapper>
</SCTitleBarContent>
            }}
<ScCartBackdrop data-testid='cart-header-backdrop' isExpanded={isExpanded} onClick={toggleExpanded} />
</SCTitleBarWrapper>
    ];
};

export { CartHeader };

```

```typescript
import React, { useContext } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useTitleBar } from 'contexts/titleBarContext';
import { InsuranceAppContext } from 'contexts/InsuranceAppContext';
import { InsuranceAppService } from 'api/InsuranceAppService';
import { useEditCoverageModal } from 'components/atoms/EditCoverageModal';

import { SCTitleBarContent, SCTitleBarWrapper } from './styles';
import { CartHeader } from './CartHeader';
import { TitleBarContent } from './TitleBarContent';
import { ITitleBarComponentProps, ITitleBarProps } from './types';

const TitleBarComponent = ({
contextCopy,
descriptionCopy,
secondaryContextCopy,
secondaryDescriptionCopy,
isTitleBarInUse,
canExpandSummary,
canEditCoverage,
onEditCoverage,
coverageValue,
paymentValue,
applicantName,
productName,
riders = [],
}: ITitleBarComponentProps) => {
if (canExpandSummary) {
return (
<CartHeader
contextCopy={contextCopy}
descriptionCopy={descriptionCopy}
secondaryContextCopy={secondaryContextCopy}
secondaryDescriptionCopy={secondaryDescriptionCopy}
isTitleBarInUse={isTitleBarInUse}
canExpandSummary={canExpandSummary}
canEditCoverage={canEditCoverage}
onEditCoverage={onEditCoverage}
coverageValue={coverageValue}
paymentValue={paymentValue}
applicantName={applicantName}
productName={productName}
riders={riders}
/>
        };
}

    return (
<SCTitleBarWrapper isTitleBarInUse={isTitleBarInUse} data-testid='title-bar-test-id'>
{contextCopy && (
<SCTitleBarContent>
<TitleBarContent
contextCopy={contextCopy}
descriptionCopy={descriptionCopy}
secondaryContextCopy={secondaryContextCopy}
secondaryDescriptionCopy={secondaryDescriptionCopy}
hasButton={false}
                    [>
</SCTitleBarContent>
            }}
</SCTitleBarWrapper>
    ];
};

const TitleBar = ({
titleBarKey,
secondaryTitleBarKey,
getAppQuote = InsuranceAppService.getAppQuote,
}: ITitleBarProps): JSX.Element | null => {
const { handleGetTitleBarCopy, isTitleBarInUse } = useTitleBar();
const titleBarCopy = handleGetTitleBarCopy(titleBarKey) || {};
const {
contextCopy,
descriptionCopy,
coverageValue,
paymentValue,
applicantName,
productName,
riders = [],
} = titleBarCopy;
const secondary = secondaryTitleBarKey === undefined ? undefined : handleGetTitleBarCopy(secondaryTitleBarKey);
const { insuranceAppResponse } = useContext(InsuranceAppContext);
const { insuranceAppId } = useParams<{ insuranceAppId: string }>();

const editFinalRatesAllowedstatus = insuranceAppResponse?.externalApp?.editFinalRatesAllowedstatus;
const canExpandSummary = editFinalRatesAllowedstatus === 'enabled' ll editFinalRatesAllowedstatus === 'disabled';
const canEditCoverage = editFinalRatesAllowedStatus === 'enabled';

    const showEditCoverageModal = useEditCoverageModal({
insuranceAppId,
getAppQuote,
    }};

    return (
<TitleBarComponent
contextCopy={contextCopy}
descriptionCopy={descriptionCopy}
secondaryContextCopy={secondary?.contextCopy}
secondaryDescriptionCopy={secondary?.descriptionCopy}
isTitleBarInUse={isTitleBarInUse}
canExpandSummary={canExpandSummary}
canEditCoverage={canEditCoverage}
onEditCoverage={showEditCoverageModal}
coverageValue={coverageValue}
paymentValue={paymentValue}
applicantName={applicantName}
productName={productName}
riders={riders}
        [>
    ];
};

export { TitleBar, TitleBarComponent };
```

```typescript
import React, { useContext, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
LoanApplicationEntities as InsuranceAppEntities,
NotificationContext,
DynamicNotification,
ProgressItemList,
ProgressBar,
Markdown,
} from 'commonui';
import { TenantContext } from 'contexts/TenantContext';
import { Helmet } from 'react-helmet';
import { useSpring, useSpringRef, useTrail, a, useChain } from 'react-spring';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { analyticsUtils, AnalyticsEvent } from 'services/Analytics';
import { Footer } from 'components/molecules/Footer';
import { SafetyNetLoading } from 'components/molecules/SafetyNetLoading';
import { FormTemplateType } from 'types/customTypes';
import { useTitleBar } from 'contexts/titleBarContext';
import { InsuranceAppService } from 'api/InsuranceAppService';
import { AppBrandingContext } from 'contexts/AppBrandingContext';
import { errorGeneric } from 'constants/notifications';
import { formatPhoneNumber } from 'utils/formatters';
import { ApplicationFlow } from 'components/pages/ApplicationFlow/ApplicationFlow';
import { InsuranceAppContext } from 'contexts/InsuranceAppContext';
import { SegmentFlowContext } from 'contexts/SegmentFlowContext';
import { SCFormWrap, SCOuterWrapper } from 'components/pages/ApplicationFlow/styles';
import { getCaptchaToken } from 'utils/recaptcha';
import {
SCDynamicFormMain,
SCDynamicFormProgress,
SCNotificationWrap,
SCProgressBarWrap,
SCWrapper,
SCSectionBody,
SCSectionHeader,
SCSectionTitle,
SCFormGrid,
SCFormBody,
SCExposition,
SCPanel,
getFloatInStyles,
} from '../DynamicFormSimple/styles';
import { ListedRoutes } from '../../Router/ListedRoutes';
import { ICustomSegmentProps } from './types';

export const CustomSegment = ({ segmentCode, children }: ICustomSegmentProps): JSX.Element => {
const {
branding: { contactPhone },
} = useContext(TenantContext);
const notificationContext = useContext(NotificationContext);
const { addNotification, clearAllDisplayNotifications } = notificationContext;
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
const { isTitleBarInUse } = useTitleBar();

    const { phoneNumber } = useContext(AppBrandingContext);
const contactPhoneNumber = formatPhoneNumber(phoneNumber || contactPhone, false);
const { insuranceAppResponse, setInsuranceAppResponse } = useContext(InsuranceAppContext);
const { coreProduct, showSafetyNetLoading } = useContext(SegmentFlowContext);
const nonEmptyExpositions =
insuranceAppResponse?.segment?.exposition?.filter((item) => Boolean(item.content)) ??
const floatInStyles = getFloatInStyles(false);
const formBodyAnimation = useSpring({
opacity: 1,
delay: 0,
connfig: { clamp: true, mass: 200 },
    }};
const numberOfExpositions = Number(nonEmptyExpositions.length);
const animatedExpositionsRef = useSpringRef();
const animatedExpositions = useTrail(numberOfExpositions, {
...floatInStyles,
trail: 150,
ref: animatedExpositionsRef,
    }};
const dfFloatInStyleRef = useSpringRef();
const animatedTimestampRef = useSpringRef();
const showFooterVersionOnly =
insuranceAppResponse?.insuranceForm.formTemplateType === FormTemplateType.AGENT_ASSISTED;

    useChain(
[animatedTimestampRef, animatedExpositionsRef, dfFloatInStyleRef],
[0.15, 0.3, 0.3 + numberOfExpositions * 0.15]
    ];

    const { insuranceAppId } = useParams<{ insuranceAppId: string }>();

    // google analytics
useEffect(() => {
if (insuranceAppResponse?.segment) {
analyticsUtils.fireApplicationEvent(AnalyticsEvent.APPLICATION_FUNNEL,insuranceAppResponse);
}
}, [insuranceAppResponse]);

const handleProgress = async (params: InsuranceAppEntities.IHandleNavigationPayload): Promise<void> => {
if (!insuranceAppId || !insuranceAppResponse) {
return;
}
const {
progress: { currentSection, currentSegment },
} = insuranceAppResponse;

        const navRequest: InsuranceAppEntities.IApplicationCallPayload = {
...params,
dataValues: [],
currentSection,
currentSegment,
};

        try {
const captchaToken = await getCaptchaToken('navigate');
const resp = await InsuranceAppService.updateInsuranceAppSegment(
insuranceAppId,
navRequest,
'none',
captchaToken
            };
clearAllDisplayNotifications(ListedRoutes.app);
setInsuranceAppResponse(resp);
} catch (err) {
addNotification(errorGeneric(ListedRoutes.app, contactPhoneNumber));
}
};

const handleProgresswithTracking = async (params: InsuranceAppEntities.IHandleNavigationPayload) => {
if (!insuranceAppResponse) return;

        const foundSegment = insuranceAppResponse.progress.progressItemList
.filter((item) => item.sectionNumber === params.requestedSection)
.flatMap((item) => item.segments || [])
.find((segment) => segment.segmentNumber === params.requestedSegment);

const navigationTitle = foundSegment?.navigationTitle ? foundSegment.navigationTitle.replace(/&/g, 'and') : -';

        analyticsUtils.fireProgressItemListClickTracker(
AnalyticsEvent.PROGRESS_ITEM_LIST_BUTTON,
insuranceAppResponse,
navigationTitle,
'Clicked'
        };

        await handleProgress(params);
};

    if (insuranceAppResponse?.segment?.segmentCode !== segmentCode) {
return <ApplicationFlow />;
}

    const {
progress: { progressItemList, segmentsComplete, segmentsCount },
} = insuranceAppResponse;

const activeSection = progressItemList?.find(({ segments }) => segments?.find(({ active )=> active));

    const wrappedProgressBar = (id: string): JSX.Element => (
<SCProgressBarWrap>
<ProgressBar id={id} completedsteps={segmentsComplete ?? o} totalsteps={segmentsCount ?? 0} />
</SCProgressBarWrap>
    ];

    return (
<SCOuterWrapper>
<Helmet>
<title>{t('PAGE_TITLE__LIFE_APPLICATION')}</title>
</Helmet>
<SCFormWrap isTitleBarInUse={isTitleBarInUse}>
<SCDynamicFormProgress>
<ProgressItemList
progressItemList={progressItemList}
formIsDirty={false}
handleNavigation={handleProgressWithTracking}
shouldScrollIntoView={false}
disableBeforeUnload={false}
disabled={!!showSafetyNetLoading}
                    [>
<SCWrapper>{wrappedProgressBar('progress-bar')}</SCWrapper>
</SCDynamicFormProgress>
<SCDynamicFormMain $isInIframe={false}>
<SCSectionBody aria-live='polite' aria-atomic='true'>
<SCSectionHeader>
{activeSection?.progressItemTitle && (
<SCSectionTitle id={`${activeSection?.progressItemCode}-TITLE`}>
{activeSection.progressItemTitle}
</SCSectionTitle>
                             }}
{wrappedProgressBar('progress-bar-xl')}
</SCSectionHeader>
{showSafetyNetLoading && (
<SafetyNetLoading
id='application-safety-net-loading'
productSpec={coreProduct?.specificationJSON || ''}
showInApp
                            [>
                        ]}
{!showSafetyNetLoading && (
                            >>
<SCNotificationWrap>
<DynamicNotification limit={3} display={ListedRoutes.app} />
</SCNotificationWrap>
<SCFormGrid>
<SCFormBody style={formBodyAnimation}>
{animatedExpositions.map((style, index) => {
const { order, content } = nonEmptyExpositions[index]:
return (
<a.div key={order}>
<SCExposition
id={${segmentCode}-EXPOSITION-${order}'}
displayBlock={content.includes('\n')}
data-testid='exposition-content'>
<SCPanel bubbleBorder style={style} className='Panel'>
<Markdown text={content} />
</SCPanel>
</SCExposition>
</a.div>
                                             ];
                                         }}}
<div id={segmentCode}>
<div>{children}</div>
</div>
</SCFormBody>
</SCFormGrid>
                            >[>
                        ]}
</SCSectionBody>
<div style={{ marginTop: '16px' }}>
<Footer showVersionOnly={showFooterVersionOnly} />
</div>
</SCDynamicFormMain>
</SCFormWrap>
</SCOuterWrapper>
    ];
};

```

### 129.‘C:\DevCode\DIxUI\src\components\organisms\DynamicFormSimple\custom-handlersInaicReplacementTypeHandler.ts'

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\DIxUI\src\components\organisms\DynamicFormSimple\custom-handlerslnaicReplacementTypeHandler.ts" type=".ts" size="1697" lines="47" chars="1697" -->
```typescript
import { DynamicFieldCustomHandler } from '../types';

const NAIC_REPLACEMENT_DETAILS_HIDDEN_FIELD = 'GROUP-NAIC-REPLACEMENT-DETAILS-HIDDEN-FIELD';

const INTERNAL_CARRIER_NAMES = new Set([
'cmfg life insurance company',
'cuna mutual group',
'members life insurance company',
'trustage',
]};

const isInternalCarrier = (insurerName: string | undefined): boolean => {
if (!insurerName) return false;
return INTERNAL_CARRIER_NAMES.has(insurerName.toLowerCase().trim());
};

export const naicReplacementTypeHandler: DynamicFieldCustomHandler = {
associatedFields: new Set([NAIC_REPLACEMENT_DETAILS_HIDDEN_FIELD]),
handleInputChange: ({ value }, { setFormData }) => {
if (!value) return;

        try {
const entries: Record<string, string>[] = JSON.parse(value);
const updatedEntries = entries.map((entry) => {
if (entry.insurerName) {
const expectedType = isInternalCarrier(entry.insurerName) ? 'internal' : 'external';
if (entry.replacementType !== expectedType) {
return { ...entry, replacementType: expectedType };
}
}
return entry;
            }];

            const modified = updatedEntries.some((updated, i) => updated !== entries[i]);

            if (modified) {
setFormData((prev) => ({
...prev,
[NAIC_REPLACEMENT_DETAILS_HIDDEN_FIELD]: JSON.stringify(updatedEntries),
                }}];
}
} catch {
// If JSON parsing fails, do nothing
}
},
};

```
<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_END -->

```typescript
import { DynamicFieldCustomHandler } from '../types';

COnst NON_NAIC_REPLACEMENT_DETAILS_HIDDEN_FIELD = 'GROUP-NON-NAIC-REPLACEMENT-DETAILS-HIDDEN-FIELD';
```

### 131. ‘C:\DevCode\DIxuI\src\components\organisms\DynamicFormSimple\custom-handlerslregistry.ts'

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START -->
```typescript
import {
CustomHandlerBlurChange,
CustomHandlerInputChange,
CustomHandlerSubmit,
DynamicFieldCustomHandler,
} from '../types';
import { naicReplacementTypeHandler } from './naicReplacementTypeHandler';
import { nonNaicReplacementTypeHandler } from './nonNaicReplacementTypeHandler';
import { routingNumberHandler } from './routingNumberHandler';

const handlers: DynamicFieldCustomHandler[] = [
routingNumberHandler,
naicReplacementTypeHandler,
nonNaicReplacementTypeHandler,
];

const inputChangeHandlerMap = new Map<string, CustomHandlerInputChange[]>();
const inputBlurHandlerMap = new Map<string, CustomHandlerBlurChange[]>();
const submitHandlerMap = new Map<string, CustomHandlerSubmit[]>();

handlers.forEach((handler) => {
handler.associatedFields.forEach((fieldCode) => {
if (handler.handleInputChange) {
if (!inputChangeHandlerMap.has(fieldCode)) {
inputChangeHandlerMap.set(fieldCode, []);
}
inputChangeHandlerMap.get(fieldCode)?.push(handler.handleInputChange);
}

        if (handler.handleInputBlur) {
if (!inputBlurHandlerMap.has(fieldCode)) {
inputBlurHandlerMap.set(fieldCode, []);
}
inputBlurHandlerMap.get(fieldCode)?.push(handler.handleInputBlur);
}

        if (handler.handleSubmit) {
if (!submitHandlerMap.has(fieldCode)) {
submitHandlerMap.set(fieldCode, []);
}
submitHandlerMap.get(fieldCode)?.push(handler.handleSubmit);
}
    }};
}};

export const findInputChangeHandlers = (fieldCode: string): CustomHandlerInputChange[] => {
return inputChangeHandlerMap.get(fieldCode) || [];
};

export const findInputBlurHandlers = (fieldCode: string): CustomHandlerBlurChange[] => {
return inputBlurHandlerMap.get(fieldCode) || [];
};

export const findSubmitHandlers = (fieldCodes: string[]): CustomHandlerSubmit[] => {
const handlers: CustomHandlerSubmit[] = [];
fieldCodes.forEach((fieldCode) => {
const fieldHandlers = submitHandlerMap.get(fieldCode);
if (fieldHandlers) {
handlers.push(...fieldHandlers);
}
    }};
return handlers;
};

```
<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_END -->

### 132. ‘C:\DevCode\DIxuI\src\components\organisms\DynamicFormSimple\custom-handlers\routingNumberHandler.ts'

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\DIxUI\src\components\organisms\DynamicFormSimple\custom-handlerslroutingNumberHandler.ts" type=".ts" size="2942" lines="64" chars="2942" -->
```typescript
import { FieldCode } from 'types/customTypes';
import { InsuranceAppService } from 'api/InsuranceAppService';
import { DynamicFieldCustomHandler } from '../types';
import { findDataField, produceModifiedDataField } from '../utils';

export const routingNumberHandler: DynamicFieldCustomHandler = {
associatedFields: new Set([FieldCode.PAYMENT_ROUTING_NUMBER]),
    [>>
* Clears the FI name when the input changes.
     >[
handleInputChange: (_event, { segment, setSegment }) => {
const dataField = findDataField(segment, FieldCode.PAYMENT_ROUTING_NUMBER);
if (dataField?.exposition?.length) {
const newSegment = produceModifiedDataField(segment, FieldCode.PAYMENT_ROUTING_NUMBER,(dataField) => {
// eslint-disable-next-line no-param-reassign
dataField.exposition = [];
            }];
setSegment(() => newSegment);
}
},
    [>>
* Calls IA async to lookup FI information based on the routing number.
* If the routing number is disallowed or invalid, sets a custom API error.
* If valid, updates the exposition with the FI name.
     >[
handleInputBlur:({ value }, { segment, customApiFieldErrors, setSegment, setCustomApiFieldErrors, t ) => {
const updateFieldLabel = async () => {
let expositionContent = '';
if (value) {
const fiInfo = await InsuranceAppService.getRoutingNumberFiInfo(value);
if ('disallowed' in fiInfo) {
const customApiError = t('PAYMENT__ROUTING_NUM_DISALLOWED');
setCustomApiFieldErrors({
...customApiFieldErrors,
[FieldCode.PAYMENT_ROUTING_NUMBER]: customApiError,
                    }};
} else if ('status' in fiInfo) {
const customApiError = t('PAYMENT__ROUTING_NUM_INVALID');
setCustomApiFieldErrors({
...customApiFieldErrors,
[FieldCode.PAYMENT_ROUTING_NUMBER]: customApiError,
                    }};
} else {
const { name: fiName } = fiInfo;
expositionContent = t('PAYMENT__ASSOCIATED_ROUTING_NUMBER', { fiName });
}
}
const newSegment = produceModifiedDataField(segment, FieldCode.PAYMENT_ROUTING_NUMBER,(dataField) => {
// eslint-disable-next-line no-param-reassign
dataField.exposition = expositionContent
                    ] [
{
order: 1,
content: expositionContent,
},
]
: [];
            }];
setSegment(() => newSegment);
};
updateFieldLabel();
},
};

```

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import {
IDynamicInputFieldProps,
useInputField,
getSharedInputProps,
IInputWrapperProps,
MaskedInput,
FieldDefinition,
} from 'commonui';
import { FieldType } from 'types/customTypes';
import { DynamicExpositionInputwrapper  from'components/molecules/DynamicExpositionInputwrapper';

export const accountNumberFieldDefinition: FieldDefinition = {
fieldType: FieldType.ACCOUNT_NUMBER,
InputComponent: (props: IDynamicInputFieldProps): JSX.Element => {
const inputFieldProps = useInputField(props);
const inputWrapperProps: IInputWrapperProps = {
...props,
showErrorIconInsideInput: true,
labelAsText: undefined,
labelIcon: undefined,
hideLabel: false,
};

        const sharedInputProps = getSharedInputProps(props, inputWrapperProps, inputFieldProps);

        return (
<DynamicExpositionInputWrapper {...inputFieldProps} {...inputWrapperProps}>
<MaskedInput {...sharedInputProps} fieldType='integer' inputMode='numeric' />
</DynamicExpositionInputWrapper>
        };
},
};
```

### 134.'C:\DevCode\DIxUI\src\components\organisms\DynamicFormSimple\fieldDefinitions\InfoPartialMarkdow

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\DIxUI\src\components\organisms\DynamicFormSimple\fieldDefinitions\InfoPartialMarkdown.tsx" type=".tsx" size="4696" lines="145" chars="4696" -->
```typescript
import React, { useCallback } from 'react';
import { IDynamicInputFieldProps, FieldDefinition, useConfirmModal, Markdown } from 'commonui';
import styled from 'styled-components';

const SCInfoPartialMarkdown = styled.div`
p {
margin: 0;
display: inline;
}
`;

const SCLinkButton = styled.button`
background: none;
border: none;
padding: 0;
color: #000000;
text-decoration: underline;
cursor: pointer;
font: inherit;

    &:hover {
text-decoration: none;
}
`;

const SCModalContent = styled.div`
max-height: 400px;
overflow-y: auto;
`;

interface IInfoPartialMarkdownContentProps {
fieldLabel: string;
fieldCode: string;
helpText?: string;
helpTextTitle?: string;
}

const InfoPartialMarkdownContent = ({
fieldLabel,
fieldCode,
helpText,
helpTextTitle,
}: IInfoPartialMarkdownContentProps): JSX.Element => {
const showConfirmModal = useConfirmModal();

    const handleLinkClick = useCallback(() => {
if (helpText) {
showConfirmModal({
title: helpTextTitle || '',
body: (
<SCModalContent>
<Markdown text={helpText} />
</SCModalContent>
                },
cancelBtnText: 'Close',
width: '600px',
            }];
}
}, [helpText, helpTextTitle, showConfirmModal]);

    // Find the position of bold marker (**text**)
const findBoldMarker = (text: string): { start: number; end: number; content: string } Inull => {
const startIdx = text.indexOf('**');
if (startIdx === -1) return null;
const endIdx = text.indexOf('**', startIdx + 2);
if (endIdx === -1) return null;
return {
start: startIdx,
end: endIdx + 2,
content: text.substring(startIdx + 2, endIdx),
};
};

    // Find the position of link marker ([text])
const findLinkMarker = (text: string): { start: number; end: number; content: string } Inull => {
const startIdx = text.indexOf('[');
if (startIdx === -1) return null;
const endIdx = text.indexOf(']', startIdx + 1);
if (endIdx === -1) return null;
return {
start: startIdx,
end: endIdx + 1,
content: text.substring(startIdx + 1, endIdx),
};
};

    // Parse bold markdown (**text**) and link markdown ([text])
const parseContent = (text: string): React.ReactNode[] => {
const parts: React.ReactNode[] = [];
let remaining = text;
let key = 0;

        while (remaining.length > 0) {
const boldMarker = findBoldMarker(remaining);
const linkMarker = findLinkMarker(remaining);

            if (boldMarker && (!linkMarker ll boldMarker.start <= linkMarker.start)) {
if (boldMarker.start > 0) {
parts.push(<span key={key++}>{remaining.substring(0, boldMarker.start)}</span>);
}
parts.push(<strong key={key++}>{boldMarker.content}</strong>);
remaining = remaining.substring(boldMarker.end);
} else if (linkMarker) {
if (linkMarker.start > 0) {
parts.push(<span key={key++}>{remaining.substring(0, linkMarker.start)}</span>);
}
parts.push(
<SCLinkButton key={key++} type='button' onClick={handleLinkClick}>
{linkMarker.content}
</SCLinkButton>
                };
remaining = remaining.substring(linkMarker.end);
} else {
parts.push(<span key={key++}>{remaining}</span>);
break;
}
}

        return parts;
};

    return (
<SCInfoPartialMarkdown id={fieldCode} data-testid={fieldCode}>
{parseContent(fieldLabel || '')}
</SCInfoPartialMarkdown>
    ];
};

export const infoPartialMarkdownFieldDefinition: FieldDefinition = {
fieldType: 'info-partial-markdown',
InputComponent: (props: IDynamicInputFieldProps): JSX.Element => {
const { dataField } = props;
const { fieldLabel, fieldCode, helpText, helpTextTitle } = dataField;

        return (
<InfoPartialMarkdownContent
fieldLabel={fieldLabel}
fieldCode={fieldCode}
helpText={helpText}
helpTextTitle={helpTextTitle}
/>
        };
},
};

```
<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_END -->

[>>
* Simplified version of the dynamic form, that doesn't include things like the timestamp and navigation arrows,
* Can be customized based on specific needs for the insurance flow.
>[
const DynamicFormSimple = (): JSX.Element => {
const { insuranceAppResponse } = useContext(InsuranceAppContext);
const {

handleNavigation = () => {},
loading,

navigation,
