disableBeforeUnload = false,
segmentsComplete,
segmentsCount,
apiFieldErrors,
customValidationBanner,
} = useDynamicFormContext();
const { addNotification, notificationList, clearAndSetNotifications  = useContext(NotificationContext);
const { branding } = useContext(TenantContext);
const [segment, setSegment] = useState<LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment>(initialSegment);
const { fieldGroups = [], segmentCode = '' } = segment;
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
const initialExpandedFieldGroups = useMemo(
() => expandFieldGroups(processMarkdownFields(replaceBaseURI(fieldGroups, branding?.link))),
[fieldGroups]
    ];
const { initialFormData, sanitizationWhitelist } = useMemo(
() => fieldGroupsToInitialValues(initialExpandedFieldGroups),
[segmentCode]
    ];
const [formData, setFormData] = useState(initialFormData);
const [formDataBeforeBlur, setFormDataBeforeBlur] = useState(initialFormData);
const [validateAllFields, setValidateAllFields] = useState(false);
const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
const [customApiFieldErrors, setCustomApiFieldErrors] = useState<Record<string, string> |undefined>(
apiFieldErrors
    ];
const [customErrorBanner, setCustomErrorBanner] = useState<NotificationEntities.INotification I undefined>(
customValidationBanner
    ];
const [previousAllocationMethod, setPreviousAllocationMethod] = useState<string  undefined>
initialFormData[FIELD_KEYS.ALLOCATION_METHOD]
    ];
const showConfirmModal = useConfirmModal();
const splitEvenlyConfirmModalProps = useSplitEvenlyConfirmModalProps();

const expandedFieldGroups = useMemo(() => expandFieldGroups(fieldGroups, formData), [fieldGroups, formData]);
const expandedFieldGroupCodes = expandedFieldGroups.map(({ groupCode }) => groupCode).join(',');
const analyticsDataFieldMap: Map<string, IAnalyticsDataField> = useMemo(
        [] =>
new Map(
expandedFieldGroups
.flatMap((fg) => (fg.dataFields as IAnalyticsDataField[]) ?? [])
.filter((df) => df.googleAnalytics)
.map((df) => [df.fieldCode, df])
            },
[expandedFieldGroups]
    ];
const dataFieldMap: Map<string, LoanApplicationEntities.IDataField> = useMemo(
() => new Map(expandedFieldGroups.flatMap((fg) => fg.dataFields ?? [J).map((df) =>[df.fieldCode, df])),
[expandedFieldGroups]
    ];

    const validationSchema = useMemo(
() => generateValidationSchema(expandedFieldGroups, t),
[expandedFieldGroupCodes, t]
    ];
const validationErrors = validateFormData(formData, validationSchema);
const isBeneficiaryManualMode = formData[FIELD_KEYS.ALLOCATION_METHOD] === ALLOCATION_METHOD.MANUAL;

    const { errors: weightErrors, hasErrors: hasMissingWeight } = validateWeight(formData, t);

const { errors: healthFieldErrors, hasErrors: hasHealthQuestionMismatch } = validateHealthQuestions(
formData,
expandedFieldGroups,
t
    ];

const { errors: tawlHealthQuestionsError, hasErrors: hasTawlHealthQuestionsError } = validateTawlHealthQuestions(
formData,
t
    ];

const { errors: heightErrors, hasErrors: hasHeightErrors } = validateHeightFields(formData,t);

    const { hasPrimaryError, hasContingentError } = isBeneficiaryManualMode
? getBeneficiaryDesignationErrors(formData)
: { hasPrimaryError: false, hasContingentError: false };
const hasBeneficiaryError = hasPrimaryError || hasContingentError;

    const allocationMethod = formData[FIELD_KEYS.ALLOCATION_METHOD];
const beneficiaryJson = formData[FIELD_KEYS.HIDDEN_FIELD];

    const heightValue = getHeightValue(formData);
const heightFieldState = getHeightFieldState(heightValue);

    // Add beneficiary percentage errors and health mismatch errors to validation errors
const beneficiaryFieldErrors: Record<string, string> = {};
const modifiedValidationErrors = {
...translateValidationErrors(validationErrors),
...(Object.keys(customApiFieldErrors ?? {}).length > 0 && { ...customApiFieldErrors }),
...beneficiaryFieldErrors,
...healthFieldErrors,
...weightErrors,
...tawlHealthQuestionsError,
...heightErrors,
};
const formIsDirty = formIsEdited(initialFormData, formData);
const formIsValid = isEmpty(modifiedValidationErrors);
const isInIframe = window.self !== window.top;
const contextData = { ...(context ?? {}), repeatingGroupRemoveButtonEnabled: true };
const allBeneficiaryErrorNotifs = validationBeneficiaryErrorNotification(true, true).map((n) => ({
...n,
display: ListedRoutes.app,
    }}];

const formInvalidNotifIsVisible = !!notificationList.find((n) => isEqual(n, errorFormInvalid()));
const beneficiaryNotifIsVisible = notificationList.some((n) =>
allBeneficiaryErrorNotifs.some((bn: any) => isEqual(n, bn))
    ];
const fieldGroupsWithoutCTA = replaceBaseURI(getFieldGroupsWithoutCTA(fieldGroups, formData), branding?.link);
const ctaFieldGroup = fieldGroups[fieldGroups.length - 1] ?? { dataFields: [] };
const formLayoutColumns = getFormNumberofColumns({ ...segment, fieldGroups: fieldGroupsWithoutCTA, segmentCode });
const nonEmptyExpositions = segment?.exposition?.filter((item) => Boolean(item.content)) ??
const nonEmptyPostExpositions = segment?.postExposition?.filter((item) => Boolean(item.content)) ?? [l;
const activesection = progressItemlist?.find(({ segments }) => segments?.find(({ active )=> active));
const mode = isDarkMode(fieldGroupsWithoutCTA[0]?.groupType);
const longerLoadSegments = [
SegmentCode.ADDITIONAL_INFO,
SegmentCode.PAYMENT_DETAILS,
SegmentCode.PAYMENT_DETAILS_CC,
SegmentCode.REVIEW_APP,
];
const isLongerLoadSegment = longerLoadSegments.includes(segment?.segmentCode as SegmentCode);
const showFooterVersionOnly =
insuranceAppResponse?.insuranceForm.formTemplateType === FormTemplateType.AGENT_ASSISTED;

    const showVoidAppModal = useVoidAppModal(addNotification, setLoading || (() => {}));
const showSaveAndCloseModal = useSaveAndCloseModal({
getRecordingStatus: () => {
returnInsuranceAppService.getCurrentVoiceEvent(insuranceAppResponse?.insuranceAppId ?? -');
},
insuranceAppId: insuranceAppResponse?.insuranceAppId ?? '',
    }};
const showEmailConsentModal = useEmailConsentModal({
insuranceAppId: insuranceAppResponse?.insuranceAppId ?? '',
addNotification,
    }};
const customHandlerContext: CustomHandlerContext = {
segment,
        formData,
        customApiFieldErrors,
        setSegment,
        setFormData,
        setCustomApiFieldErrors,
        showConfirmModal,
        t.
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        setCustomApiFieldErrors(apiFieldErrors);
    },[apiFieldErrors]);

    // Generic form validation notifications
    useEffect(() => {
        // set generic form invalid notification (only if no beneficiary errors exist)
if (!formIsValid && validateAllFields && !formInvalidNotifIsVisible &&!hasBeneficiaryError){
            if (!customErrorBanner){
                clearAndSetNotifications([...notificationList, errorFormInvalid()J);
            else{
                clearAndSetNotifications([...notificationList, customErrorBannerJ);
            }
        }

        // remove generic form invalid notification
        if (formIsValid && validateAllFields && formInvalidNotifIsVisible) {
            if (!customErrorBanner)
                clearAndSetNotifications(removeErrorFormInvalidNotif(notificationList));
            else{
clearAndSetNotifications(notificationList.filter((n) => !isEqual(n, customErrorBanner)));
            }
        }
}, [formIsValid, validateAllFields, formInvalidNotifIsVisible, customErrorBanner, hasBeneficiaryError);

    // Beneficiary-specific error notifications
    useEffect(() => {
const shouldshowBeneficiaryErrors = !customErrorBanner && isBeneficiaryManualMode && hasBeneficiaryError;
        const shouldRemoveBeneficiaryErrors =
!customErrorBanner && beneficiaryNotifIsVisible && (!hasBeneficiaryError ll!isBeneficiaryManualMode);

        if (shouldShowBeneficiaryErrors && (validateAllFields ll !beneficiaryNotifIsVisible)) {
const beneficiaryNotifs = validationBeneficiaryErrorNotification(hasPrimaryError, hasContingentError).map(
                (n) => ({ ...n, display: ListedRoutes.app )
            };
// Remove both generic error and beneficiary errors, then add only beneficiary errors
            const baseList = notificationList.filter(
(n) => !isEqual(n, errorFormInvalid()) && !allBeneficiaryErrorNotifs.some((bn) => isEqual(n, bn))
            };
            clearAndSetNotifications([...baseList, ...beneficiaryNotifs]):
        else if (shouldRemoveBeneficiaryErrors){
            clearAndSetNotifications(
notificationList.filter((n) => !allBeneficiaryErrorNotifs.some((bn) => isEqual(n, bn)))
            };
        }
    }, [
        validateAllFields,
        beneficiaryNotifIsVisible,
        hasBeneficiaryError,
        isBeneficiaryManualMode,
        hasPrimaryError,
        hasContingentError,
        customErrorBanner,
    ]};

    /* CommonuI recalculates beneficiary allocations when the allocation method changes.
     * This causes stale percentage allocation if the user never toggles allocation method.
     * This re-runs the allocation calculation in CommonuI whenever the beneficiary JsoN
     * changes and synchronizes the hidden field if calculation allocations differ.
     >[
    useEffect(() => {
        const updatedBeneficiaries = getSyncedBeneficiaryAllocations(formData);

        if (updatedBeneficiaries){
            setFormData((prev) => ({
                 ...prev.
                [FIELD_KEYS.HIDDEN_FIELD]: updatedBeneficiaries,
            }]};
        }
      , [allocationMethod, beneficiaryJson]);

    // Health Questions CheckBox error notification
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!addNotification) return;
const exists = notificationList.some((n) => n.title === t('ERROR_HEALTH_NOTIFICATION_TITLE'));
        if (hasHealthQuestionMismatch && !exists) {
            addNotification(
                type: NotificationEntities.NotificationType.ERRoR,
                display: ListedRoutes.app,
                dismissable: false,
                title: t('ERROR_HEALTH_NOTIFICATION_TITLE')
                message: t('ERROR_HEALTH_NOTIFICATION_MESSAGE'),
            }];
        }
        if (!hasHealthQuestionMismatch && exists) {
clearAndSetNotifications(notificationList.filter((n) => n.title !== t('ERROR_HEALTH_NOTIFICATION_TITLE')));
        }
    },[hasHealthQuestionMismatch]);

    // Sets placeholder text for any of our current weight fields
    useEffect(() => {
        setSegment(configureweightField(initialSegment));
    },[initialSegment]);

    // google analytics for initial load
    useEffect(() => {
        if (insuranceAppResponse?.segment) {
analyticsUtils.fireApplicationEvent(AnalyticsEvent.APPLICATION_FUNNEL,insuranceAppResponse);
            if (!insuranceAppResponse.segment.isComplete) {
                // for initial load of every segment
                analyticsInputChangeUtils.runInitialLoadAnalytics(
                    formData,
                    analyticsDataFieldMap,
                    insuranceAppResponse
                };
                const runInitialUserDataAnalytics = async () => {
awaitanalyticsUserDataUtils.runInitialLoad(insuranceAppResponse.googleAnalyticsData);
                };
                if(
                    insuranceAppResponse.segment.sectionNumber === 1 &&
                    insuranceAppResponse.segment.segmentNumber === 1
                } {
                     // for initial load of first segment of application
                    runInitialUserDataAnalytics();
                }
            }
        }
      [insuranceAppResponse?.segment]);

const handleBulkInputChange = async (incomingData: Record<string, string>): Promise<void>
        // Special handling for beneficiary allocation method changes
        if (incomingData[FIELD_KEYS.ALLOCATION_METHOD]){
            try {
// Handle beneficiary allocation method changes with confirmation and recalculation
                const updatedFormData = await handleBeneficiaryBulkAllocationMethodChange({
                    formData,
                    bulkValues: incomingData,
                    previousAllocationMethod,
                    showConfirmModal,
                    confirmModalProps: splitEvenlyConfirmModalProps,
                }};

                setFormData(updatedFormData);
                setPreviousAllocationMethod(incomingData[FIELD_KEYS.ALLOCATION_METHODJ);
            }catch
                setCustomErrorBanner(undefined);
            }
         else{
            // Normal bulk update for fields without allocation method change
            setFormData((data) => ({ ...data, ...incomingData }));
        }
    };

    // google analytics for data field errors
    const handleShowError = (name: string, error?: string) => {
        if (name.toLowerCase() === 'useCombinedPdfViewer'.toLowerCase()) {
analyticsUtils.fireApplicationEvent(AnalyticsEvent.ERROR_LOADING_DOC,insuranceAppResponse);
        } else
analyticsUtils.fireFieldErrorEvent(AnalyticsEvent.FIELD_ERROR, name, error ?? -', insuranceAppResponse);
        }
    };
const submitForm = async (payload: typeof formData, readonly: boolean): Promise<boolean> =>
if (hasHealthQuestionMismatch Il hasHeightErrors Il hasMissingWeight Il hasTawlHealthQuestionsError){
            setSubmitting(false);
            return false;
        }
        if (readonly ll formIsValid) {
            try {
                const customHandlers = findSubmitHandlers([...dataFieldMap.keys()J);
                let updatedPayload = payload;
                for (let i = O; i < customHandlers.length; i++) {
                    const customHandler = customHandlers[i];
                    // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
                    updatedPayload = await customHandler({
                         ..customHandlerContext,
                        formData: updatedPayload,
                    }};
                }
                setCustomErrorBanner(undefined);
                onSubmit(sanitizeFormData(updatedPayload, sanitizationwhitelist));
            catch
                setSubmitting(false);
                return false;
            }
         }else{
            globalThis.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                const firstInvalidInputField = document.querySelector(
                     'input.invalid, .invalid input'
                ) as HTMLElement | null;
                firstInvalidInputField?.focus();
            }];
        }
        return true;
    };

    const handleSubmit = async (value = 'save'): Promise<void> => {
        setSubmitting(true);
        const submitCode = fieldGroups?.[fieldGroups.length - 1]?.dataFields?.[o].fieldCode;
        let payload = { ...formData };
        if(submitCode){
            payload = {
                ...payload,
                [submitCode]: value,
            };
        }
        switch((value){
            case 'save':
            case 'submit': {
                if (formData[FieldCode.APPREVIEW_EMAIL_DISCLOSURE_CONSENT] === 'false'){
                    try {
                        await showEmailConsentModal();
                    } catch {
                        // User chose to continue the application; no further action required.
                    }
                    break;
                }
                if(!readonly){
                    setValidateAllFields(true);
                }
                await submitForm(payload, readonly);
                break;
            }
            case 'back':
                setCustomErrorBanner(undefined);
                handleNavigation({ action: 'Back' );
                break;

            case 'void':
                showVoidAppModal?.();
                break;

            case 'close':
                try {
                    await showSaveAndcloseModal?.():
                } catch {
                    // User canceled confirmation modal; no further action required.
                }
                break;

            default:
                setCustomErrorBanner(undefined);
                // Ignore the validation and submit the form (Includes single type question).
                onSubmit(sanitizeFormData(payload, sanitizationwhitelist));
        }

        setSubmitting(false);
    };

    const handleBeforeUnload = (_next: Location, _history: Action): void => {
        analyticsUtils.fireApplicationEvent(AnalyticsEvent.ABANDoN_APP, insuranceAppResponse):
    };

    [>
     * Callback function used for all inputs fields.
     * On input change will return the field name and field value as strings
     >[
    const handleInputChange = async (
        name: string.
        value: string,
        args?: LoanApplicationEntities.IDataFieldChangeArgs
    ): Promise<void> => {
        const clearCustomFieldError = () => {
            if (customApiFieldErrors && name in customApiFieldErrors) {
                const newCustomFieldErrors = { ...customApiFieldErrors };
                delete newCustomFieldErrors[name];
                setCustomApiFieldErrors(newCustomFieldErrors);
            }
        };

        const runAnalyticsIfNeeded = (formDataToUse: Record<string, string>) => {
            const analyticsDataField = analyticsDataFieldMap.get(name);
            if(
                analyticsDataField?.googleAnalytics &&
                analyticsInputChangeUtils.shouldTrackInputChange(analyticsDataField, args)
            } {
                analyticsInputChangeUtils.runAnalytics(
                    name,
                    value,
                    formDataToUse,
                    analyticsDataFieldMap,
                    insuranceAppResponse,
                    false,
                    false
                };
            }
        };

        if (name === FIELD_KEYS.ALLOCATION_METHOD) {
            try {
                clearCustomFieldError();
                const updatedFormData = await handleBeneficiaryAllocationMethodChange({
                    formData,
                    fieldName: name,
                    fieldValue: value,
                    previousAllocationMethod,
                    showConfirmModal,
                    confirmModalProps: splitEvenlyConfirmModalProps,
                }};
                setFormData(updatedFormData);
                setPreviousAllocationMethod(value);
                runAnalyticsIfNeeded(updatedFormData):
             catch {
                setCustomErrorBanner(undefined);
            }
            return;
        }

        if (isHealthQuestionOptionField(name)){
            const parentCode = name.replace('-OPTIONs', -');
            const parentYesValue =
dataFieldMap.get(parentCode)?.options?.find((option) => option.label ==='Yes')?.value ?? 'Yes';

            const hasOptionSelected = hasOptionSelections(value);

            if (hasOptionSelected && formData[parentCode] !== parentYesValue {
                setFormData((prevData) =>({
                     ...prevData,
                     [parentCode]: parentYesValue,
                }}];
            }
        }

        clearCustomFieldError();
        setFormData((prevData: Record<string, string>) => ({
            ...prevData,
            [name]: value,
        }]};
        runAnalyticsIfNeeded(formData);
        const customHandlers = findInputChangeHandlers(name);
        customHandlers.forEach((handler) => {
            handler({ name, value, args , customHandlerContext);
        }];
    };

    [>
     Callback function used for all inputs fields.
     * Input blur will return the field name and field value as strings
     >[
    const handleBlur = (name: string, value: string, args?: any): void => {
        const customHandlers = findInputBlurHandlers(name);
        customHandlers.forEach((handler) => {
            handler({ name, value, args }, customHandlerContext);
        }];
        analyticsInputChangeutils.runAnalytics(
            name,
            value,
            formDataBeforeBlur,
            analyticsDataFieldMap,
            insuranceAppResponse,
            false,
            true,
            args
        };
        analyticsUserDatautils.runInputChange(name, value, modifiedValidationErrors);
        setFormDataBeforeBlur(formData);
    };

    const handleButtonClick = useCallback(
        (name: string, value?: string): void => {
analyticsButtonclickutils.runAnalytics(dataFieldMap, insuranceAppResponse, name, value):
        },
        [segment]
    ];

    const handleNavigationwithTracking = useCallback(
        (navigationArgs: any) => {
            const foundSegment = progressItemList
                .filter((item) => item.sectionNumber === navigationArgs.requestedSection)
                .flatMap((item) => item.segments ll [])
                .find((segment) => segment.segmentNumber === navigationArgs.requestedSegment);

            const navigationTitle = foundSegment?.navigationTitle
                ? foundSegment.navigationTitle.replace(/&/g, 'and')
                ; ` `;

            analyticsUtils.fireProgressItemListclickTracker(
                AnalyticsEvent.PROGRESS_ITEM_LIST_BUTTON,
                insuranceAppResponse,
                navigationTitle,
                'Clicked'
            };

            handleNavigation(navigationArgs):
        },
        [handleNavigation, progressItemList, insuranceAppResponse]
    ];

    const animatedTimestampRef = useSpringRef();
    const floatInstyles = getFloatInstyles(navigation?.isUserMovingInReverse ll false);
    const floatInAnimation = useSpring({
          .floatInstyles,
        config: { tension: 120 },
    }};

    const numberofExpositions = Number(nonEmptyExpositions.length);
    const animatedExpositionsRef = useSpringRef();
    const animatedExpositions = useTrail(numberofExpositions, {
         ..floatInstyles,
        trail: 150,
        ref: animatedExpositionsRef,
    }};

    const numberofPostExpositions = Number(nonEmptyPostExpositions.length);
    const animatedPostExpositionsRef = useSpringRef();
    const animatedPostExpositions = useTrail(numberofPostExpositions, {
          ..floatInstyles,
        trail: 150,
        ref: animatedPostExpositionsRef,
    }};

    const dfFloatInStyleRef = useSpringRef():

    const formBodyAnimation = useSpring({
        opacity: loading ? 0 : 1,
        delay: loading ? 900 : 0,
        config: { clamp: true, mass: 2o0 },
    }};

    useChain(
        [animatedTimestampRef, animatedExpositionsRef, dfFloatInStyleRef,
        [0.15, 0.3, 0.3 + number0fExpositions * 0.15, 0.3 + number0fExpositions * 0.15 + 0.15]
    ];

    const wrappedProgressBar = (id: string): JsX.Element => (
        <SCProgressBarWrap>
<ProgressBar id={id} completedsteps={segmentsComplete ?? o} totalsteps={segmentsCount ?? 0} />
        </SCProgressBarWrap>
    ];

    const customFieldInventory = useMemo(() => {
        return
            [FieldType.ACCOUNT_NUMBER]: accountNumberFieldDefinition,
            'info-partial-markdown': infoPartialMarkdownFieldDefinition,
        } as Record<string, FieldDefinition>;
      [};

    // CommonuI ProgressAccordion uses hooks while mapping segment items.
    // Remount when progress structure changes to avoid hook-order mismatch crashes.
    const progressListRenderKey = useMemo(
        [] =>
            progressItemList
                .map(
                    (item)=>
'${item.sectionNumber}-${item.progressItemCode ??-'}-${item.segments?.length ?? O}-$?
                            item.isComplete ? '1' : '0'
                        }`
                }
                .join('{'),
        [progressItemList]
    ];

    return(
        >>
            {!disableBeforeUnload &&(
                <BeforeUnload
                    customDialogue
                    formIsDirty={formIsDirty}
                    formIsValid={formIsValid}
                    onSaveAndContinue={handleSubmit}
                    onBlock={handleBeforeUnload}
                    modaloverrides={{
                        width: '800px',
                        cancelBtnText: t('APP_FLOW_LEAVING_APP_FORM_MODAL__EXIT_TEXT'),
                        cancelBtnClassName: 'gtm-btn',
                        continueBtnClassName: 'gtm-btn',
                        continueBtnText: t('APP_FLOW_LEAVING_APP_FORM_MODAL__STAY'),
                        title: -',
                        ariaTitle: t('APP_FLOW_LEAVING_APP_FORM_MODAL__ARIA_TITLE'),
body: t('APP_FLOW_LEAVING_APP_FORM_MODAL__BODY_TEXT', { returnObjects: true }),
                    }}
                />
            }}
            <SCDynamicFormProgress>
                <ProgressItemList
                    key={progressListRenderKey}
                    progressItemList={progressItemList}
                    formIsDirty={formIsDirty}
                    handleNavigation={handleNavigationwithTracking}
                    disableBeforeUnload={disableBeforeUnload}
                    shouldScrollIntoView

                <sCwrapper>{wrappedProgressBar('progress-bar')}</sCwrapper>
            </SCDynamicFormProgress>
            <SCDynamicFormMain $isInIframe={isInIframe}>
                <scSectionBody aria-live='polite' aria-atomic='true'>
                    <SCSectionHeader>
                        {activeSection?.progressItemTitle && (
                            <ScSectionTitle id={'${activeSection?.progressItemCode}-TITLE'}>
                                {activeSection.progressItemTitle}
                             </SCSectionTitle>
                        ]}
                        {wrappedProgressBar('progress-bar-xl')}
                    </SCSectionHeader>
                    <SCNotificationwrap>
                        <DynamicNotification limit={3} display={ListedRoutes.app} />
                    </SCNotificationwrap>
                    <SCFormGrid>
                        {loading &&(
                            <LoadingIcon
                                backgroundColor='contrastwhite'
loadingText={isLongerLoadSegment ? t('GLOBAL__LOADING_VERIFY_INFO') : undefined)
                                fixedHeight={500}
                            [>
                        ]}
                        {!loading &&(
                            <SCFormBody style={formBodyAnimation}>
                                {nonEmptyExpositions.length > 0 &&
                                    animatedExpositions.map((style, index) => {
                                         const { order, content } = nonEmptyExpositions[index];
                                         return(
                                             <a.div key={order}>
                                                 <SCExposition
                                                     id={'${segmentCode}-EXPOSITION-${order}'}
                                                     displayBlock={content.includes('\n')}
                                                     data-testid='exposition-content'>
<SCPanel bubbleBorder style={style} className='Panel'>
                                                         <Markdown text={content} />
                                                     </SCPanel>
                                                 </SCExposition>
                                             </a.div>
                                         ];
                                     }}}
                                <div id={segmentCode}>
<div className={formLayoutColumns === 2 ? 'form-layout-two-columns' : '}>
                                         {!!fieldGroupsWithoutCTA.length && (
                                             <HistoryProvider>
                                                 <SCHeightFieldOverride
feetMissing={validateAllFields && heightFieldstate.feetMissing)
inchesMissing={validateAllFields && heightFieldstate.inchesMissing}>
                                                     <DynamicSegment
                                                         fieldGroups={fieldGroupsWithoutCTA}
                                                         handleNavigation={handleNavigation}
                                                         readonly={readonly}
                                                         mode={mode}
                                                         contextData={contextData}
                                                         formData={formData}
                                                         validateAllFields={validateAllFields}
validationErrors={modifiedValidationErrors}
handleBulkInputChange={handleBulkInputChange}
                                                         handleInputChange={handleInputChange}
                                                         handleBlur={handleBlur}
                                                         handleShowError={handleShowError}
                                                         formLayoutColumns={formLayoutColumns}
                                                         style={floatInAnimation}
                                                         handleButtonClick={handleButtonClick}
                                                         showGroupActionBtnTop
                                                         shouldUseFieldGroupMode
                                                         fieldInventory={customFieldInventory}
                                                     />
                                                 </SCHeightFieldOverride>
                                             </HistoryProvider>
                                         ]}
                                         {nonEmptyPostExpositions.length > 0 &&
                                             animatedPostExpositions.map((style, index) => {
const { order, content } = nonEmptyPostExpositions[index];
                                                 return(
                                                     <a.div
                                                         key={order}
style={index === 0 ? { marginTop: '16px'  : undefined}>
                                                         <SCExposition
id={'${segmentCode}-PoST-EXPOSITION-${order}'}
displayBlock={content.includes('\n')}
data-testid='post-exposition-content'>
<SCPanel bubbleBorder style={style} className='Panel'>
                                                                 <Markdown text={content} />
                                                             </SCPanel>
                                                         </SCExposition>
                                                     </a.div>
                                                 };
                                             }}}
                                         {ctaFieldGroup.dataFields &&(
                                             <DynamicFormButtons
                                                 dataFields={ctaFieldGroup.dataFields}
                                                 onSubmit={handleSubmit}
                                                 loading={!!loading}
                                                 readonly={submitting}
                                                 animationDelay={550}
                                                 btnClassName='gtm-btn'
                                             [>
                                         ]}
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </SCFormBody>
                        ]}
                    </SCFormGrid>
                </SCSectionBody>
                <Footer showVersiononly={showFooterVersiononly} />
            </SCDynamicFormMain>
        >[>
    ];
};

export { DynamicFormSimple };

import { IUseConfirmModalReturn, LoanApplicationEntities, NotificationEntities } from'commonui';
import { IUseTypedTranslation  from 'i18n';
import { IInsuranceApp } from 'types/customTypes';

export interface IDynamicFormsimpleProps {
    handleNavigation: (params: LoanApplicationEntities.IHandleNavigationPayload) => void;
    loading?: boolean;
    setLoading?: (loading: boolean) => void;
    navigation?: LoanApplicationEntities.INewLoanNavigation;
    progressItemList: LoanApplicationEntities.IProgressItem[l;
    onSubmit: (formData?: Record<string, string>, recordstate?: string) => void;
    segment: LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment;
    contextData?: any;
    editMode?: boolean;
    readonly: boolean;
    disableBeforeUnload?: boolean;
    segmentsCount?: number;
    segmentsComplete?: number;
    apiFieldErrors?: Record<string, string>;
    insuranceAppResponse?: IInsuranceApp;
    customValidationBanner?: NotificationEntities.INotification;
}

export type CustomHandlerInputChange = (
    event: {
        name: string:
        value: string;
        args?: LoanApplicationEntities.IDataFieldChangeArgs;
    },
    context: CustomHandlerContext
) => void;

export type CustomHandlerBlurChange = (
    event: {
        name: string;
        value: string;
        args?: any;
    },
    context: CustomHandlerContext
=> void;

export type CustomHandlerSubmit = (context: CustomHandlerContext) => Promise<Record<string,string>>;

export interface CustomHandlerContext
    segment: LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment;
    formData: Record<string, string>;
    customApiFieldErrors?: Record<string, string>;
    setSegment: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment>>;
    setFormData: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Record<string, string>>>;
setCustomApiFieldErrors: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Record<string, string> l undefined>>;
    showConfirmModal: IUseConfirmModalReturn;
    t: IUseTypedTranslation['t'];
}

export interface DynamicFieldCustomHandler
    associatedFields: Set<string>;
    handleInputChange?: CustomHandlerInputChange;
    handleInputBlur?: CustomHandlerBlurChange;
    handleSubmit?: CustomHandlerSubmit;
}

export interface CustomHandlerRegistry {
    findInputChangeHandlers: (fieldCode: string) => CustomHandlerInputChange[l;
    findInputBlurHandlers: (fieldCode: string) => CustomHandlerBlurChange[J;
    findSubmitHandlers: (fieldCodes: string[J) => CustomHandlerSubmit[];
}

import { ALLOCATION_METHOD, calculateBeneficiaryAllocations, FIELD_KEYS, LoanApplicationEntities  from 'commonui';
import { produce } from 'immer';

[>>
* Find a data field in a segment by its field code.
>[
export const findDataField = (
    segment: LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment,
    fieldCode: string
): LoanApplicationEntities.IDataField | undefined => {
    const findField = (fieldGroup?: (typeof segment.fieldGroups)[oj) =>
fieldGroup?.dataFields?.find(({ fieldCode: targetFieldCode ) => targetFieldCode === fieldCode);
    const fieldGroup = segment.fieldGroups.find((fieldGroup) => !!findField(fieldGroup));
    return findField(fieldGroup);
};

[>>
* Convert markdown bold (**text**) syntax in info-partial field labels to HTML bold tags.
* This allows bold text to render inline with regular text.
>[
export const parseMarkdownBold = (text: string): string => {
    return text.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
};

[>>
* Process field groups to transform info-partial-markdown fields.
* Converts markdown bold syntax in fieldLabel to HTML for proper rendering.
>[
export const processMarkdownFields = (
    data: LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup[]
): LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup[] => {
    return data?.map((group: LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup) => ({
        ...group.
        dataFields: group.dataFields?.map((field: any) => {
            if (field.fieldType === 'info-partial-markdown' && field.fieldLabel) {
                return {
                      ..field,
                    fieldLabel: parseMarkdownBold(field.fieldLabel),
                };
            }
            return field;
        }],
    }}];
};

export function replaceBaseURI(data: LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup[l, baseURI: string) {
    // Strip https:// prefix for display text (but keep it for actual URLs in hrefs)
    const baseURIWithoutHttps = baseURI.replace(/^https:\/\//, -');

    return data?.map((group: LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup) => ({
        ...group.
        dataFields: group.dataFields?.map((field: any) => {
            const updatedField = { ...field };

if (typeof updatedField.helpText === 'string' && updatedField.helpText.includes('{{baseURI}}')){
updatedField.helpText = updatedField.helpText.replaceAll('{{baseURI}}', baseURI);
            }

if (typeof updatedField.value === 'string' && updatedField.value.includes('{{baseURI}}')){
                updatedField.value = updatedField.value.replaceAll('{{baseURI}}', baseURI);
            }

if (typeof updatedField.fieldLabel === 'string' && updatedField.fieldLabel.includes('{{baseURI}}')){
// For fieldLabel, replace {{baseuRI}} in link text (between [ and J) without https://,
                // but keep https:// in href (between ( and ))
                let updatedLabel = updatedField.fieldLabel;

                // Replace {{baseURI}} in markdown link text with URL without https://
                // Pattern: [{{baseURI}}/path] -> [www.trustage.com/path]
updatedLabel = updatedLabel.replace(/\[\{\{baseURI\}\}([^\]]*)\]/g, '[${baseURIWithoutHttps}$1]');

                // Replace {{baseURI}} in href with full URL including https:/
                // Pattern: ({{baseURI}}/path) -> (https://www.trustage.com/path)
updatedLabel = updatedLabel.replace(/\(\{\{baseURI\}\}([^)j*)\)/g, "(${baseURI}$1)");

                // Also handle any remaining {{baseURI}} that aren't in markdown links
                updatedLabel = updatedLabel.replaceAll('{{baseURI}}', baseURI);

                updatedField.fieldLabel = updatedLabel;
            }

            return updatedField;
        }],
    }}];
}
[>>
* Change the actual spec of a data field (as opposed to just the value.)
* Should be used with immer, as it modifies the segment in place.
>[
const modifyDataField = (
    segment: LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment,
    fieldCode: string,
    modifier: (dataField: LoanApplicationEntities.IDataField) => void
] => {
    const dataField = findDataField(segment, fieldCode);
    if(dataField){
        modifier(dataField);
    }
};

[>>
* Produce a new segment with a single modified data field.
>[
export const produceModifiedDataField = (
    segment: LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment,
    fieldCode: string,
    modifier: (dataField: LoanApplicationEntities.IDataField) => void
  LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment => {
    return produce(segment, (draftSegment) => {
        modifyDataField(draftSegment, fieldCode, modifier);
    }};
};

[>>

* with custom ones we want within DIxUI.
* @param validationErrors The current map of validation errors on the form.

>[
export const translateValidationErrors: (validationErrors: Record<string, string>) => Record<string, string> = (
    validationErrors
] => {
    const ret = validationErrors;
    [>
     * Custom translations for the wyoming form.
     * Three is the max number of surrender charge entries allowed.
     >[
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++){
        if (ret['FIELD-EXISTING-COVERAGE-SURRENDER-CHARGE-AMOUNT-WYOMING-${i}'J) {
            ret['FIELD-EXISTING-COVERAGE-SURRENDER-CHARGE-AMOUNT-WYOMING-${i}'] =
                'You must provide a percentage or dollar amount'
        }
    }

    return ret;
};

export const getSyncedBeneficiaryAllocations = (formData: Record<string, string>): string lnull => {
    if (formData[FIELD_KEYS.ALLOCATION_METHOD] !== ALLOCATION_METHOD.SPLIT_EVENLY {
        return null;
    }

    const recalculatedBeneficiaries = calculateBeneficiaryAllocations(formData);

if (!recalculatedBeneficiaries ll recalculatedBeneficiaries === formData[FIELD_KEYS.HIDDEN_FIELDJ){
        return null;
    }

    return recalculatedBeneficiaries;
};

import { LoanApplicationEntities } from 'commonui';
import { produceModifiedDataField } from './utils';

interface ValidationResult {
    errors: Record<string, string>;
    hasErrors: boolean;
}

const createValidationResult = (errors: Record<string, string>): ValidationResult => ({
    errors,
    hasErrors: Object.keys(errors).length > 0,
}};

eXpOrt cOnst HEIGHT_FIELDS = ['FIELD-HEALTH-QUESTION-height', 'FIELD-HEALTH-QUESTION-HEIGHT'J;
eXpOrt cOnst WEIGHT_FIELDS = ['FIELD-HEALTH-QUESTION-Weight', 'FIELD-HEALTH-QUESTION-WEIGHT'J;

export const getHeightValue = (formData: Record<string, string>): string I undefined =>
    formData['FIELD-HEALTH-QUESTION-height'] ?? formData['FIELD-HEALTH-QUESTION-HEIGHT'];

export const getHeightFieldstate = (value?: string) => {
    try {
        const parsed = JsoN.parse(value ll '{}');

        return {
            feetMissing: !parsed.feet?.trim(),
            inchesMissing: !parsed.inches?.trim(),
        };
    } catch {
        return
            feetMissing: true,
            inchesMissing: true,
        };
    }
};

export const validateHeight = (value: string, t: any): string I undefined => {
    try {
        const parsed = JsoN.parse(value ll '{}');
        const feet = parsed.feet?.trim();
        const inches = parsed.inches?.trim():

        if (!feet ll !inches){
            return t('ERROR__HEIGHT_REQUIRED');
        }
    catch {
        return t('ERROR_HEIGHT_INVALID_FORMAT');
    }
    return undefined;
};

export const validateHeightFields = (formData: Record<string, string>, t: any): ValidationResult => {
    const errors: Record<string, string> = {;

    HEIGHT_FIELDS.forEach((fieldCode) => {
        const value = formData[fieldCode];

        if (!value){
            return;
        }

        const error = validateHeight(value, t);

        if (error){
            errors[fieldCode] = error;
        }
    }};

    return createValidationResult(errors);
};

export const isHealthQuestionoptionField = (fieldCode: string): boolean =>
    fieldCode.startsWith('FIELD-HEALTH-QUESTION-') && fieldCode.endsWith('-OPTIONS');

export const hasoptionSelections = (optionValue: unknown): boolean => {
    if (Array.isArray(optionValue)) {
        return optionValue.length > o;
    }

    if (typeof optionValue !== 'string') {
        return false;
    }

    try {
        const parsed = JsoN.parse(optionValue);
        if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed)) {
            return Object.values(parsed).some(Boolean);
        }
    catch {
        // Ignore lint no-empty error
    }

    return optionValue.trim().length > o;
};

export const validateHealthQuestions = (
    formData: Record<string, any>,
    fieldGroups: LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup[],
    t: any
): ValidationResult => {
    const errors: Record<string, string> = {};

    fieldGroups.forEach((group) => {
        group.dataFields?.forEach(({ fieldCode }) => {
            if (!isHealthQuestionoptionField(fieldCode)){
                return;
            }

            const parentCode = fieldCode.replace('-OPTIoNs', -');

const isNegativeAnswer = formData[parentCode] === 'No' |l formData[parentCode] ==='false';

            const hasSelectedoptions = hasOptionSelections(formData[fieldCode]);

            if ((isNegativeAnswer && hasSelectedoptions) {
                errors[parentCode] = t('ERROR__HEALTH_PARENT_MISMATCH');

                errors[fieldCode] = t('ERROR_HEALTH_OPTIONS_MISMATCH');
            }
        }];
    }};

    return createValidationResult(errors);
};

export const validateTawlHealthQuestions = (formData: Record<string, any>, t: any): ValidationResult => {
    const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
    const parentCode = 'FIELD-HEALTH-QUESTION-MED-PAST5YEARS-A';
    const OptionsCode = 'FIELD-HEALTH-QUESTION-MED-PAST5YEARS-A-OPTIONS';

    const answeredYes = formData[parentCode] === 'Yes';
    const optionSelected = hasOptionSelections(formData[optionsCode]);

    if (answeredYes && !optionSelected) {
        errors[optionsCode] = t('DYNAMIC_FORM_ERROR_REQUIRED_SELECT_OPTION');
    }

    return createValidationResult(errors);
};

export const configureweightField = (
    segment: LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment
): LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment => {
    let updatedSegment = segment;

    WEIGHT_FIELDS.forEach((fieldCode) => {
        updatedSegment = produceModifiedDataField(updatedSegment, fieldCode, (field) => {
            // eslint-disable-next-line no-param-reassign
            field.placeholder = 'Enter weight in pounds';
            // eslint-disable-next-line no-param-reassign
            field.required = true;
        }];
    }};
    return updatedSegment;
};

export const validateweight = (formData: Record<string, any>, t: any): ValidationResult => {
    const errors: Record<string, string> = {};

    const weightField = WEIGHT_FIELDS.find((fieldCode) => formData[fieldCode] !== undefined);

    if (weightField && !formData[weightFieldj?.trim()) {
        errors[weightField] = t('DYNAMIC_FORM__ERROR_REQUIRED_TEXT_FIELD', {
            article: 'your weight',
        }];
    }

    return createValidationResult(errors);
};

import React, { useContext } from 'react';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { CardTag } from 'components/atoms/CardTag';
import { Rate } from 'components/molecules/Rate';
import { RateCrossSell  from 'components/molecules/RateCrossSell';
import { formatMoney } from 'utils/formatters';
import { CardTagType } from 'types/customTypes';
import { SCFinalRateCard, SCDivider, SCTotalContainer, SCTotalLabel, SCTotalAmount } from'./styles';
import { FinalRateContext, IFinalRateCardModel } from '../../../contexts/FinalRateContext';

export const FinalRateCard = (): JSX.Element  null => {
    const cardModel = useContext(FinalRateContext);
    if(!cardModel){
        return null;
    }
    const {
        primary,
        showApproved,
        riders,
        totalPayment,
        billingFreq,
        crossSellModuleTitle = -',
        finalRateLearnMoreModalTitle = -',
        finalRateLearnMoreModalSubTitle = '
        finalRateLearnMoreModalBody = -',
    } = cardModel as IFinalRateCardModel;
    const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
    const primaryCardHeader = t('FINAL_RATE_PRODUCT_HEADER');

    return(
        <SCFinalRateCard hasTag={showApproved} id='final-rate-card'>
            {showApproved && <CardTag id='final-rate-card-tag' type={CardTagType.APPROvED} />}
            <Rate
                id='final-rate-card-primary'
                cardHeader={primaryCardHeader}
                currentIndex={primary.currentIndex}
                valuesList={primary.valuesList}
                onIncrementclick={() => primary.handleInputChange(primary.currentIndex + 1)}
                onDecrementClick={() => primary.handleInputChange(primary.currentIndex - 1)}
                coverageAmount={primary.coverageAmount}
                billingFreq={billingFreq}

            {riders.list.map((rider) => {
                return(
                    <React.Fragment key={rider.name}>
                        <SCDivider />
                        {rider.selected ? (
                            <Rate
                                id={'final-rate-rider-${rider.name}'}
                                cardHeader={rider.name}
                                currentIndex={rider.currentIndex}
                                valuesList={rider.valuesList}
                                coverageAmount={rider.coverage}
learnMoreText={'${finalRateLearnMoreModalTitle}\n\n${finalRateLearnMoreModalSubTitle}\n\n${finalRateLearnMoreModalBody
                                onIncrementClick={() => riders.handleInputChange(rider.order,
onDecrementClick={() => riders.handleInputChange(rider.order, rider.currentIndex - 1)}
                                onRemoveBtnClick={() => riders.handleAddRemove(rider.order))
                                billingFreq={billingFreq}
                            [>
                        ] ; [
                            <RateCrossSell
                                id={'final-rate-cross-sell-rider-${rider.name}'}
                                cardHeader={rider.name}
                                moduleTitle={crossSellModuleTitle}
learnMoreText=${finalRateLearnMoreModalTitle}\n\n${finalRateLearnMoreModalSubTitle}\n\n${finalRateLearnMoreModalBody
                                currentIndex={rider.currentIndex}
                                valuesList={rider.valuesList}
                                onAddBtnClick={() => riders.handleAddRemove(rider.order, true)}
                                coverageAmount={rider.coverage}
                                billingFreq={billingFreq}
                            [>
                        ]}
                    </React.Fragment>
                };
            }]}
            {totalPayment !== undefined && (
                >>
                    <SCDivider isLastDivider />
                    <SCTotalContainer>
<SCTotalLabel>{t('FINAL_RATE_TOTAL_LABEL', { frequency: billingFreq })}</SCTotalLabel>
                        <SCTotalAmount>{formatMoney(totalPayment)}</SCTotalAmount>
                    </SCTotalContainer>
                >[>
            }}
        </SCFinalRateCard>
    ];
};

import React, { useRef, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, usestate } from 'react';
import { ModalContext, useConfirmModal } from 'commonui';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { IInactivityTimeoutProps } from './types';
import { ONE_MINUTE, MODAL_TIMEOUT, INACTIVITY_TIMEOUT  from './constants';

export const InactivityTimeout = ({
    onTimeout,
    onRenew,
    resetInactivityTimer,
}: IInactivityTimeoutProps): JSX.Element => {
    const showConfirmModal = useConfirmModal();
    const { t  = useTypedTranslation();
    const { closeHigherOrderModal } = useContext(ModalContext);
    const [timeInactivity, setTimeInactivity] = usestate(o); // in milliseconds
    const [timedout, setTimedout] = usestate(false)
    const timeoutDuration: number = INACTIVITY_TIMEoUT;

    const timerRef = useRef({ timeout: O, isModalopen: false });

    const clearTimeoutResetRef = (): void => {
        clearTimeout(timerRef.current.timeout);
        timerRef.current.timeout = O;
        timerRef.current.isModalopen = false;
    };

    const handleInvalidateSession = useCallback((): void => {
        closeHigherOrderModal();
        clearTimeoutResetRef();
        onTimeout();
        setTimedout(true);
        sessionstorage.clear();
    },[closeHigherOrderModal, onTimeout]);

    const handleRenew = useCallback((): void => {
        onRenew();
        setTimeInactivity(0);
        clearTimeoutResetRef();
    },,[onRenew]);

    const handleInactivity = useCallback(async (): Promise<void> => {
        try {
            timerRef.current.timeout = setTimeout(() => {
                handleInvalidateSession();
            }, MODAL_TIMEOUT) as unknown as number;
            if (!timerRef.current.isModalopen) {
                timerRef.current.isModalopen = true;
                await showConfirmModal({
                    title: t('INACTIVITY_TIMEOUT_MODAL__TITLE'),
                    body: t('INACTIVITY_TIMEOUT_MODAL__BODY', {
                        minutes: ‘${INACTIVITY_TIMEOUT / ONE_MINUTE}',
                        returnobjects: true,
                        joinArrays: 'Inin',
                    }},
                    isHigherOrderModal: true,
                    continueBtnText: t('INACTIVITY_TIMEOUT_MODAL__BUTTON_CONT'),
                    onContinueBtnclick: (): void => {
                        handleRenew();
                    },
                    onclose: (): void => {
                        handleRenew();
                    },
                }};
            }
         } catch(err){
            clearTimeoutResetRef();
        }
    }, [handleInvalidateSession, showConfirmModal, t, handleRenew]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (resetInactivityTimer){
            setTimeInactivity(0);
        }
       [resetInactivityTimer]);

    useEffect(() => {
        const intervalId = setInterval(() => {
            setTimeInactivity((prevTime) => prevTime + 1000);
        {, 1000);
        return () => clearInterval(intervalId);
    },[setTimeInactivity]);

if (timeInactivity >= timeoutDuration - MoDAL_TIMEoUT && !timerRef.current.isModalOpen &&!timedout){
        handleInactivity();
    }

    return <div data-testid='inactivity-timeout-root' />;
};

import React from 'react';
import { DynamicFormButtons, DisplayMode } from 'commonui';
import { ApplicationFlow } from 'components/pages/ApplicationFlow/ApplicationFlow';
import { CustomSegment } from 'components/organisms/CustomSegment/CustomSegment';
import { LoadingIcon } from 'components/atoms/LoadingIcon';
import { InsuranceAppContext } from 'contexts/InsuranceAppContext';
import { SegmentCode } from 'types/customTypes';
import {
    SCDynamicSegment,
    SCDynamicForm,
    SCFieldGroup,
    SCGroupTitle,
    SCDisclaimer,
    ScCheckboxwrapper,
    SCCheckbox,
    SCErrorMessage,
    SCCancel,
} from './styles';
import { useTermsAndConditions } from './useTermsAndConditions';

export const TermsAndConditions = (): JSX.Element => {
    const insuranceCtx = React.useContext(InsuranceAppContext);
    const resolvedInsuranceAppResponse = insuranceCtx.insuranceAppResponse;
    const insuranceContextValue = React.useMemo(
        () => ({ ...insuranceCtx, insuranceAppResponse: resolvedInsuranceAppResponse ),
        [insuranceCtx, resolvedInsuranceAppResponse]
    ];
    const {
        loading,
        insuranceAppResp,
        productsubtype,
        onSubmit,
        submitting.
        termsGroupContent: {
            termsFieldGroup,
            checkedBoxes,
            checkboxFieldData,
            errors,
            handleCheckboxClick,
            handleCancelclick,
            ctaDataFields,
            cancelBtnText,
            refs,
            showCancelAppLink,
        },
        otherGroupContent: {
            otherFieldGroups,
            handleInputChange,
            otherFormData,
            validationErrors,
            formLayoutColumns,
            handleButtonClick,
            handleShowError,
        },
    } = useTermsAndConditions();

    if (insuranceAppResp?.segment?.segmentCode !== SegmentCode.TERMS_CONDITIONS) {
        return <ApplicationFlow />;
    }

    return(
        <InsuranceAppContext.Provider value={insuranceContextValue}>
            <CustomSegment segmentCode={SegmentCode.TERMS_CONDITIONS}>
                {loading && <LoadingIcon backgroundColor='contrastwhite' fixedHeight={5oo} />}
                {!loading &&(
                    >>
                        {otherFieldGroups.length > 0 && (
                            <SCDynamicSegment
                                fieldGroups={otherFieldGroups}
                                handleNavigation={() => {}}
                                 readonly={false}
                                mode={DisplayMode.LIGHT}
                                contextData={{}}
                                formData={otherFormData}
                                validateAllFields={submitting}
                                validationErrors={validationErrors}
                                handleInputChange={handleInputChange}
                                formLayoutColumns={formLayoutColumns}
                                handleButtonClick={handleButtonClick}
                                handleShowError={handleShowError}
                                $product={productSubtype}
                                style={{}}
                                showGroupActionBtnTop
                            [>
                        ]}
                        {termsFieldGroup &&(
                            <SCDynamicForm>
                                <SCFieldGroup>
                                    <fieldset>
                                         <legend>
<SCGroupTitle>{termsFieldGroup.groupTitle}</SCGroupTitle>
                                         </legend>
                                         <SCDisclaimer text={termsFieldGroup.groupSubTitle} />
                                         <SCCheckboxWrapper>
                                             {checkboxFieldData.map(
                                                 [{
                                                     field,
                                                     fieldCode,
                                                     fieldLabel,
                                                     errorMessage,
                                                     ariaLabel,
                                                     disabled,
                                                 =>
                                                     return(
                                                         <React.Fragment key={field}>
                                                             <SCCheckbox
                                                                 ref={refs[field]}
                                                                 id={fieldCode}
                                                                 checked={checkedBoxes[field]}
className={errors[field] ?'invalid' : -'}
                                                                 l...(errors[field] && ↑
                                                                     'aria-invalid': true,
'aria-describedby':'${field}-checkbox-error',
                                                                 }]}
onChange={() => handleCheckboxClick(field)}
                                                                 arialabel={arialabel}
                                                                 aria-describedby={
errors[field] ? '${field}-checkbox-error' : -
                                                                 }
                                                                 disabled={disabled}>
                                                                 <p>{fieldLabel}</p>
                                                             </SCCheckbox>
                                                             {errors[field] &&(
                                                                 <SCErrorMessage
id={'${field}-checkbox-error}
                                                                     error={errorMessage}
                                                                     showIcon
                                                                 [>
                                                             ]}
                                                         </React.Fragment>
                                                     };
                                                 }
                                             ]}
                                             {showCancelAppLink &&(
<ScCancel onClick={handleCancelclick}>{cancelBtnText}</ScCancel>
                                             ]}
                                         </ScCheckboxWrapper>
                                    </fieldset>
                                </SCFieldGroup>
                            </SCDynamicForm>
                        ]}
                        {ctaDataFields &&(
                             <DynamicFormButtons
                                dataFields={ctaDataFields}
                                onSubmit={onSubmit}
                                loading={!!loading}
                                 readonly={false}
                                animationDelay={550}
                                btnclassName='gtm-btn'
                            [>
                        ]}
                    >[>
                }}
            </CustomSegment>
        </InsuranceAppContext.Provider>
    ];
};

refs: Record<keyof TermsAndConditionsCheckboxes, React.Refobject<HTMLInputElement I null>>;

}

            const submitCode = fieldGroups?.[fieldGroups.length - 1]?.dataFields?.[o].fieldCode;
            const transformedInitialFormData = Object.fromEntries(
Object.entries(initialFormData).map(([key, value]) => [key, ‘base64,${btoa(value)}'])
            };
            let payload = {
                 ..transformedInitialFormData,
                  ..otherFormData,
                   .(checkedBoxes.authorization !== undefined && {
                     [FieldCode.ELECTRONIC_TERMS_AUTHORIZATION]: ‘${checkedBoxes.authorization}',
                }},

                   .(checkedBoxes.documents !== undefined && {
                     [FieldCode.ELECTRONIC_TERMS_DOCUMENTS_CONSENT]:‘${checkedBoxesS.documents}',
                }},
                   (initialFormData[FieldCode.ELECTRONIC_TERMS_CANCEL_APP] !== undefined && {
                     [FieldCode.ELECTRONIC_TERMS_CANCEL_APP]: -',
                }},
            };
            if(submitCode){
                payload = {
                     ...payload,
                     [submitCode]: value,
                };
            }
            const requestValues = Object.entries(payload).map((entry) => {
                return { fieldCode: entry[o], value: entry[1] };
            }];
            setLoading(true);
            const navRequest: InsuranceAppEntities.IApplicationCallPayload = {
                action: 'SeekToEnd',
                dataValues: requestValues,
                currentSection,
                currentSegment,
            };
            const captchaToken = await getCaptchaToken('navigate');
            const resp = await InsuranceAppService.updateInsuranceAppSegment(
                insuranceAppId,
                navRequest,
                'save'.
                captchaToken
            };
            clearAllDisplayNotifications(ListedRoutes.app);
            setInsuranceAppResp(resp);
            setInsuranceAppResponse(resp);
            setLoading(false);
        } catch (err){
            setLoading(false);
            const appResultError: ApiAppError = isJsonstring(err.message)
                ? JsoN.parse(err.message).payload?.appResult
                 : undefined;
            if (appResultError){
                if (appResultError.redirect) {
                    if (appResultError?.redirectUrl) {
                        window.location.href = appResultError?.redirectUrl;
                        return;
                    }
                    navigate(generatePath(ListedRoutes.summary, { insuranceAppId }));
                } else if (!appResultError.bannerTitle && !appResultError.bannerMessage) {
                    addNotification(errorApplicationGeneric(phoneNumber ll contactPhone));
                else {
addNotification(errorApplication(appResultError, contactPhoneNumber, insuranceAppResp));
                }
             else{
                addNotification(errorApplicationGeneric(phoneNumber ll contactPhone));
            }
        }
    };

    return {
        loading,
        insuranceAppResp,
        productSubtype,
        onSubmit,
        submitting.
        termsGroupContent:
            termsFieldGroup,
            checkedBoxes,
            checkboxFieldData,
            errors,
            handleCheckboxClick,
            handleCancelclick,
            ctaDataFields,
            cancelBtnText,
            showCancelAppLink,
            refs,
        },
        otherGroupContent: {
            otherFieldGroups,
            handleInputChange,
            otherFormData,
            validationErrors,
            formLayoutColumns,
            handleButtonClick,
            handleShowError,
        },
    };
}

const handleNavigation = async (params: InsuranceAppEntities.IHandleNavigationPayload):Promise<void> => {

};

            case ProductSubType.GAWL:
                productName = nonHookTypedTranslation('APP_FLOW_TITLE_BAR_HEADER_GAWL_COPY');
                break;
            case ProductSubType.TAWL:
                productName =
                    formTemplateType === FormTemplateType.AGENT_ASSISTED
                        ? nonHookTypedTranslation('APP_FLOW_TITLE_BAR_HEADER_AGENT_TAWL_COPY')
                         : nonHookTypedTranslation('APP_FLOW_TITLE_BAR_HEADER_TAWL_COPY');
                break;
            default:
        }
    }

    return
        coverageValue: formatMoney(coverage, false),
        paymentValue: payment ? ‘$(payment}${frequencyDisplay}' : ',
        applicantName,
        productName,
    };
};

export const getTitleBarRiders = (
    insuranceApp: IInsuranceApp
): Array<{ name: string; coverageValue: string; paymentValue: string }> => {
    if (!insuranceApp?.externalApp?.riders ll insuranceApp.externalApp.riders.length === 0) {
        return [];
    }

    const frequency = insuranceApp.externalApp.billingFreq ?? -';
const { text: frequencyText, separator: frequencySeparator } = getFrequencyFormat(frequency);
    const frequencyDisplay = '${frequencySeparator}${frequencyText.toLowerCase()}';

    return insuranceApp.externalApp.riders.map((rider) => {
        const coverageValue = formatMoney(rider.coverageAmount, false);
const paymentValue = rider.premiumAmount ? '${rider.premiumAmount}${frequencyDisplay}'

        return {
            name: rider.name,
            coverageValue,
            paymentValue,
        };
    }};
};

export const checkAndRenderNotifications = (
    insuranceApp: IInsuranceApp,
    clearAndSetNotifications: (notifications: NotificationEntities.INotification[l) => void,
    phoneNumber: string
] => {
    let notificationsList = [] as NotificationEntities.INotification[l;

    // segment specific banners
    if (insuranceApp?.externalApp?.banners) {
        notificationsList = [
             ...notificationsList,
               . insuranceApp.externalApp.banners.map((banner) =>
                segmentSpecificNotification(ListedRoutes.app, banner, phoneNumber, insuranceApp)
            },
        ];
    }

    // coverage confirmation notification
    const showCoverageConfirmationSegments = SegmentCode.COVERAGE_ADJUSTMENT;
if (showCoverageConfirmationSegments === (insuranceApp?.segment.segmentCode as SegmentCode)){
        const productMaxCoverage = insuranceApp?.externalApp?.productMaxCoverageAmount ?? 0;
        const userAvailableCoverage = insuranceApp?.externalApp?.coverageMaximumAmount ?? O;
        const userCurrentCoverage = productMaxCoverage - userAvailableCoverage;

        const productName =
insuranceApp.externalApp?.contentAuthoring?.productName ?? insuranceApp?.coreProduct?.name ?? -';
        notificationsList = [
             ...notificationsList,
            coverageConfirmationMessage(
                ListedRoutes.app,
                productName,
                formatMoney(userCurrentCoverage),
                formatMoney(productMaxCoverage),
                formatMoney(userAvailableCoverage)
            },
        ];
    }

    let estateFullName = -';
if ((insuranceApp?.externalApp?.beneficiaries ?? [j).some((b) => b.relationship ==='ESTATE')){
        estateFullName = insuranceApp?.externalApp?.insuredFullName;
    }

    const estateMsg = nonHookTypedTranslation('APP_FLOW_NOTIFICATION_BENEFICIARIES_ESTATE', {
        estateFullName,
    }};
    const beneficiariesNameList: stringl] = [];
    const beneficiaries = insuranceApp?.externalApp?.beneficiaries ?? [l;

    beneficiaries.forEach((b) => {
        if (b.relationship === 'ESTATE') {
            beneficiariesNameList.push(estateMsg);
        else{
            const name = b.orgName Il b.name?.firstName;
            if (name) beneficiariesNameList.push(name);
        }
    }};

    let beneficiariesText = -';
    if (beneficiariesNameList.length > 0) {
beneficiariesText = new Intl.ListFormat('en-us', { style: 'long', type: 'conjunction' }).format(
            beneficiariesNameList
        };
    }
const hideBnfNotifSegments = getSegmentswithoutBeneficiariesNotif(insuranceApp?.coreProduct?.subtype ?? ');
if (beneficiariesText && !hideBnfNotifSegments.includes(insuranceApp?.segment?.segmentCodeas SegmentCode)){
notificationsList = [...notificationsList, beneficiariesMessage(ListedRoutes.app, beneficiariesText)l;
    }

    const paymentSegmentCodes = [SegmentCode.PAYMENT_DETAILS, SegmentCode.PAYMENT_DETAILS_CC]:
    if(
        insuranceApp?.externalApp?.isFirstMonthPaymentOneDollar &&
        insuranceApp?.coreProduct?.subtype?.toLowerCase() === ProductSubType.SIwL &&
        paymentSegmentCodes.includes(insuranceApp.segment?.segmentCode as SegmentCode)
    ] {
        notificationsList.push(
            segmentSpecificNotification(
                ListedRoutes.app,
                {
                    type: 'success',
title:nonHookTypedTranslation('APP_FLOW__NOTIFICATION_FIRST_MONTH_ONE_DOLLAR_TITLE'),
message:nonHookTypedTranslation('APP_FLOW__NOTIFICATION_FIRST_MONTH_ONE_DOLLAR_MESSAGE'),
                    dismissable: false,
                },
                phoneNumber,
                insuranceApp
            }
        };
    }

    clearAndSetNotifications(notificationsList);
};

export const getAppSafetyNetLoadingData = (
    decisionstate: Decisionstate,
    insuranceAppResp?: IInsuranceApp
]; {
    showSafetyNetLoading: boolean;
    decisionStateToSet: DecisionState;
    decisionIsResolved: (resp: IInsuranceApp) => boolean;
    pollingInterval: number I null;
    pollingEndpoint: (insuranceAppId: string) => Promise<IInsuranceApp>;
} => {
    let showSafetyNetLoading = false;
    let decisionstateToSet = Decisionstate.Resolved;
    let decisionIsResolved = ((_: IInsuranceApp) => false;
    const pollingEndpoint = (insuranceAppId: string): Promise<IInsuranceApp> =>
        getCaptchaToken('read').then((captchaToken) =>
            InsuranceAppService.getInsuranceApp(insuranceAppId, captchaToken)
        };
    if (!insuranceAppResp)
        return {
            showSafetyNetLoading.
            decisionStateToSet,
            decisionIsResolved,
            pollingInterval: null,
            pollingEndpoint,
        };
    const { segmentCode } = insuranceAppResp.segment;
    const product = insuranceAppResp.coreProduct.subtype?.toLowerCase();
const isAgentForm = insuranceAppResp.insuranceForm.formTemplateType === FormTemplateType.AGENT_ASSISTED;
    const pollingInterval = decisionStateNeedsPolling(decisionState) ? POLLING_INTERVAL : null;
    switch (segmentCode){
        case SegmentCode.FINAL_RATE: {
            const isAllowedProduct = [ProductSubType.TAwL].includes(product as ProductSubType);
            const underwritingstatusIsPending =
insuranceAppResp.externalApp.underwritingStatus === UnderwritingStatus.PENDING
insuranceAppResp.externalApp.underwritingstatus === UnderwritingStatus.INCOMPLETE;
            if (underwritingStatusIsPending){
                decisionStateToSet = DecisionState.Loading;
            }
            decisionIsResolved = (resp: IInsuranceApp) => {
                return(
                     !!resp.externalApp.underwritingstatus &&
                    resp.externalApp.underwritingStatus !== UnderwritingStatus.PENDING &&
                    resp.externalApp.underwritingStatus !== UnderwritingStatus.INCOMPLETE
                };
            };
const allowSafetyNetPolling = !insuranceAppResp.segment.isComplete && isAllowedProduct && !isAgentForm;
            showSafetyNetLoading =
allowSafetyNetPolling && underwritingStatusIsPending && decisionstateNeedsPolling(decisionstate);
            break;
        }
        default:
    }

    // set localstorage so we only show the safety net loading screen once per segment
    const lsSafetyNetLoading = LSService.getItem(LS_CONSTANTS.APP_SAFETY_NET_LOADING);
    let lsSafetyNetLoadingParsed = lsSafetyNetLoading ? JsoN.parse(lsSafetyNetLoading) : {};
    if(
        lsSafetyNetLoadingParsed?.insuranceAppId === insuranceAppResp.insuranceAppId &&
        lsSafetyNetLoadingParsed?.[segmentCode]
    ] {
        showSafetyNetLoading = false;
    }
    if (showSafetyNetLoading){
        lsSafetyNetLoadingParsed = {
              ..lsSafetyNetLoadingParsed,
            insuranceAppId: insuranceAppResp.insuranceAppId,
            [segmentCode]: true,
        };
LSSerVice.Set(LS_CONSTANTS.APP_SAFETY_NET_LOADING,JsoN.stringify(lsSafetyNetLoadingParsed));
    }

    return
        showSafetyNetLoading,
        decisionStateToSet,
        decisionIsResolved,
        pollingInterval,
        pollingEndpoint,
    };
};

export const getFinalRateRiderCrossSellModuleTitle = (insuranceAppResponse: IInsuranceApp):string => {
const crossSellModuleTitle = insuranceAppResponse.externalApp?.contentAuthoring?.crossSellModuleTitle ll ';
    return crossSellModuleTitle;
};

export const getFinalRateRiderCrossSellModal = (insuranceAppResponse: IInsuranceApp) => {
    const finalRateLearnMoreModalTitle =
        insuranceAppResponse.externalApp?.contentAuthoring?.finalRateLearnMoreModalTitle Il -';
    const finalRateLearnMoreModalSubtitle =
insuranceAppResponse.externalApp?.contentAuthoring?.finalRateLearnMoreModalSubtitle Il
    const finalRateLearnMoreModalBody =
        insuranceAppResponse.externalApp?.contentAuthoring?.finalRateLearnMoreModalBody ll ';

    return {
        finalRateLearnMoreModalTitle,
        finalRateLearnMoreModalSubtitle,
        finalRateLearnMoreModalBody,
    };
};

import React, { useEffect, useContext, usestate  from 'react';
import { NotificationContext, DynamicNotification, helpers, LsService } from 'commonui';
import { TenantContext } from 'contexts/TenantContext';
import { Helmet } from 'react-helmet';
import { useNavigate, generatePath, useSearchParams  from 'react-router-dom';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { ss_coNsTANTs } from 'services/sessionstorage';
import { errorGeneric, errorMessage  from 'constants/notifications';
import { AppBrandingContext } from 'contexts/AppBrandingContext';
import { LoadingIcon } from 'components/atoms/LoadingIcon/LoadingIcon';
import { formatPhoneNumber } from 'utils/formatters';
import { LS_CONSTANTs } from 'services/localStorage';
import { InsuranceAppService } from 'api/InsuranceAppService';
import { InsuranceAppContext } from 'contexts/InsuranceAppContext';
import { ListedRoutes } from '../../Router/ListedRoutes';
import { SCwrapper } from './styles';

export const ApplicationResume = (): JSX.Element => {
    const {
        loading: tenantLoading,
        branding: { contactPhone },
    } = useContext(TenantContext):
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
    const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    const requestId = searchParams.get('rid') ?? -';
    const partnerId = searchParams.has('pid');
    const notificationContext = useContext(NotificationContext);
    const { clearAndSetNotifications  = notificationContext;
const { cobrandingPartner, phoneNumber, setLoading: setCobrandingLoading } = useContext(AppBrandingContext);
    const { setInsuranceAppResponse  = useContext(InsuranceAppContext);
    const [loading, setLoading] = usestate(true);
    const contactPhoneNumber = formatPhoneNumber(
        phoneNumber Il cobrandingPartner.phoneNumbers.contact Il contactPhone,
        false
    ];
    const contactName = cobrandingPartner.displayName l t('GLOBAL__TRUSTAGE');

    useEffect(() => {
        const callResumeApp = async () => {
            if (tenantLoading) return;
            if (requestId){
                if (partnerId){
setCobrandingLoading(true); // This will trigger AppBrandingContext to fetch the new cobranding partner details
                }
                try {
                    const resp = await InsuranceAppService.resumestartedApp(requestId);
                    sessionStorage.setItem(SS_CONSTANTS.SESSION_ID, resp?.sessionId ?? -');
                    LSService.removeItem(LS_CONSTANTS.APP_SAFETY_NET_LOADING);
                    // Store resume response in context so ApplicationFlow uses it directly
// This ensures the backend's voice signature override (triggered by redirectId) is preserved
                    setInsuranceAppResponse(resp);
navigate(generatePath(ListedRoutes.app, { insuranceAppId: resp.insuranceAppId ),{
                        state: resp,
                    }};
                catch(err){
                    clearAndSetNotifications([
                        errorMessage(
                            ListedRoutes.appResume,
                            t('ERROR__APP_NOT_AVAIL',{
                                contactName,
                                phoneNumber: helpers.applyInputMask(
                                    contactPhoneNumber,
                                    helpers.MASK_FORMAT['phone-number']
                                 },
                            }]
                        ],
                    ]};
                finally {
                    setLoading(false);
                }
            else{
clearAndSetNotifications([errorGeneric(ListedRoutes.appResume,contactPhoneNumber)J);
                setLoading(false):
            }
        };

        callResumeApp();
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, [requestId, tenantLoading]);

    return(
        <sCWrapper>
            <Helmet>
                <title>{t('PAGE_TITLE_LIFE_APPLICATION')}</title>
            </Helmet>
            <DynamicNotification limit={1} display={ListedRoutes.appResume} />
            {loading && <LoadingIcon />}
        </SCWrapper>
    ];
};
