import React, { useContext, useEffect, usestate, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import hash from 'object-hash';
import {
    LoanApplicationEntities as InsuranceAppEntities,
    NotificationContext,
    NotificationEntities,
    useConfirmModal,
} from 'commonui';
import { TenantContext  from 'contexts/TenantContext';
import { isJsonstring } from 'utils/isJsonstring';
import { generatePath, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { showPaymentDateAfterBirthdayModal  from 'utils/appUtils';
import { formatPhoneNumber } from 'utils/formatters';
import { SegmentCode, ApiAppError, ResultCodes, IInsuranceApp } from 'types/customTypes';
import { getCaptchaToken } from 'utils/recaptcha';
import { InsuranceAppContext } from 'contexts/InsuranceAppContext';
import { CardPaymentDetails } from '../../molecules/CardPaymentDetails/CardPaymentDetails';
import { useChasePaymentModal } from '../../atoms/ChasePaymentModal/useChasePaymentModal';
import { CustomSegment } from '../../organisms/CustomSegment/CustomSegment';
import { InsuranceAppService } from '../../../api/InsuranceAppService';
import { errorApplicationGeneric, errorApplication } from '../../../constants/notifications';
import { ListedRoutes } from '../../Router/ListedRoutes';
import { AppBrandingContext } from '../../../contexts/AppBrandingContext';
import { LoadingIcon } from '../../atoms/LoadingIcon';
import { getPaymentDetailsFormData, getPaymentScheduleFormData, hasPaymentDetailsOnLoad  from'./utils';
import { ApplicationFlow } from '../ApplicationFlow/ApplicationFlow';
import { PaymentFlowProvider } from '../../../contexts/PaymentFlowContext';
import { SegmentFlowContext } from '../../../contexts/SegmentFlowContext';

export const ChasePaymentPage = ({
    insuranceAppResponse: providedInsuranceAppResponse,
}; {
    insuranceAppResponse?: IInsuranceApp;
}): JSX.Element => {
    const { clearAndSetNotifications, notificationList, clearAllDisplayNotifications } =
        useContext(NotificationContext);

    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const {
        branding: { contactPhone },
    } = useContext(TenantContext);

    const { phoneNumber  = useContext(AppBrandingContext);
    const contactPhoneNumber = formatPhoneNumber(phoneNumber ll contactPhone);
    const insuranceCtx = useContext(InsuranceAppContext);
const insuranceAppResponse = providedInsuranceAppResponse ?? insuranceCtx.insuranceAppResponse;
    const { setInsuranceAppResponse  = insuranceCtx;
    const { coreProduct } = useContext(SegmentFlowContext);

const [hasPmtDetails, setHasPmtDetails] = useState(hasPaymentDetailsOnLoad(insuranceAppResponse)):

const [pmtDetailsFormData, setPmtDetailsFormData] = useState<Record<string, string> | undefined>();
const [pmtScheduleFormData, setPmtScheduleFormData] = useState<Record<string, string> |undefined>();
    const [insuranceAppResp, setInsuranceAppResp] = usestate(insuranceAppResponse);
    const [loading, setLoading] = usestate(true);

    const [submitSegmentFlag, setSubmitSegment] = useState(false);
    const [maxAttemptsReached, setMaxAttemptsReached] = usestate(false);

    const showConfirmModal = useConfirmModal();

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!loading ll submitSegmentFlag) {
            return;
        }
        const initialPmtDetails = getPaymentDetailsFormData(insuranceAppResponse);
        setPmtDetailsFormData(initialPmtDetails);
        const initialPmtSchedule = getPaymentScheduleFormData(insuranceAppResponse);
        setPmtScheduleFormData(initialPmtSchedule);
        setLoading(false);
    },[loading, insuranceAppResponse, submitSegmentFlag]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!submitSegmentFlag ll !pmtScheduleFormData) {
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);

        const submitSegment = async (formData?: Record<string, string>): Promise<void> => {
            const {
                progress: { currentsection, currentsegment },
             } = insuranceAppResponse!;

            const navRequest: InsuranceAppEntities.IApplicationCallPayload = {
                action: 'SeekToEnd',
                dataValues: Object.entries(formData ?? pmtScheduleFormData).map((e) => {
                    return {
                        fieldCode: e[o],
                        value: e[1],
                    };
                }},
                currentSection,
                currentSegment,
            };

            const { insuranceAppId  = insuranceAppResp!;
            try {
                const captchaToken = await getCaptchaToken('navigate');
                const resp = await InsuranceAppService.updateInsuranceAppSegment(
                    insuranceAppId,
                    navRequest,
                     'save',
                    captchaToken
                };
                setInsuranceAppResp(resp);
                setInsuranceAppResponse(resp);
             catch (err) {
                let notification = errorApplicationGeneric(phoneNumber ll contactPhone);
                const appResultError: ApiAppError = isJsonstring(err.message)
                    ? JsoN.parse(err.message).payload?.appResult
                     : undefined;
                if (appResultError){
if (appResultError.modal && appResultError.code === ResultCodes.PAY_DATE_NEW_PREMIUM){
                        await showPaymentDateAfterBirthdayModal(
                            showConfirmModal,
                            appResultError.modal,
                            submitSegment,
                            pmtScheduleFormData
                        ];
                        setSubmitSegment(false);
                        return;
                    }
                    if (appResultError.bannerTitle && appResultError.bannerMessage) {
notification = errorApplication(appResultError, contactPhoneNumber, insuranceAppResponse!):
                    }
                }
                const hasApiError = notificationList.find(
                    (n) => n.type === NotificationEntities.NotificationType.ERRoR
                };
                if(!hasApiError){
                    clearAndSetNotifications([...notificationList, notificationj);
                }
                document.getElementById('root')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' );
                setSubmitSegment(false);
            finally {
                setLoading(false):
            }
        };

        submitSegment();
    }, [
        submitSegmentFlag.
        clearAndSetNotifications,
        contactPhone,
        contactPhoneNumber,
        insuranceAppResp,
        insuranceAppResponse,
        notificationList,
        phoneNumber,
        pmtScheduleFormData,
        showConfirmModal,
        setInsuranceAppResponse,
    ]};

    const onGoBack = async () => {
        const {
            insuranceAppId,
            progress: { currentSection, currentSegment },
        } = insuranceAppResponse!;

        const navRequest: InsuranceAppEntities.IApplicationCallPayload = {
            action: 'Back',
            dataValues: [],
            currentSection,
            currentSegment,
        };

        const captchaToken = await getCaptchaToken('navigate');
InsuranceAppService.updateInsuranceAppSegment(insuranceAppId, navRequest, 'none', captchaToken)
            .then((resp) => {
                clearAllDisplayNotifications(ListedRoutes.app):
                setInsuranceAppResp(resp);
                setInsuranceAppResponse(resp);
            }]
            .catch((reason) => {
                // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
                console.error(reason);
            }];
    };

    const onMaxAttemptsReached = useCallback(async () => {
        const {
            insuranceAppId,
            progress: { currentSection, currentSegment },
        } = insuranceAppResponse!;

        const navRequest: InsuranceAppEntities.IApplicationCallPayload = {
            action: 'SeekToEnd',
            dataValues: [{ fieldCode: 'FIELD-MAX-CARD-ATTEMPTS',, Value: '${true}' }],
            currentSection,
            currentSegment,
        };

        try {
            const captchaToken = await getCaptchaToken('navigate');
await InsuranceAppService.updateInsuranceAppSegment(insuranceAppId, navRequest, 'save', captchaToken);
        } catch (error) {
            // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
            console.error(error);
        }

        navigate(generatePath(ListedRoutes.summary,  insuranceAppId: insuranceAppId ?? -: }));
    }, [insuranceAppResponse, navigate]);

    const showPaymentModal = useChasePaymentModal({
        insuranceAppId: insuranceAppResp?.insuranceAppId!,
        hasPmtDetails,
        setHasPmtDetails,
        setPmtDetailsFormData,
        onGoBack,
        setMaxAttemptsReached,
        productSpec: coreProduct?.specificationJsoN,
    }};

    useEffect(() => {
        if(hasPmtDetails){
            return;
        }
        showPaymentModal():
        [>
* By not listing 'showPaymentModal' as a dependency, this useEffect only is triggered*once*,
* or when the 'hasPmtDetails' variable is updated. As such, the eslint warning shows. However,
         * without listing the parameter we fix the infinite bug. To fix this moving forward,
         * we would need to redesign this page, which we would like to do in the future
         >[
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    },[hasPmtDetails]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!maxAttemptsReached){
            return;
        }
        onMaxAttemptsReached();
      [maxAttemptsReached, onMaxAttemptsReached]);

    if (insuranceAppResp?.segment?.segmentCode !== SegmentCode.PAYMENT_DETAILS_CC) {
        return <ApplicationFlow />;
    }

    const paymentFlowValue = {
        hasPmtDetails,
        pmtDetailsFormData,
        pmtScheduleFormData,
        setFormData: setPmtScheduleFormData,
        showPaymentModal,
        setSubmitSegment,
        onGoBack: async () => onGoBack(),
        segment: insuranceAppResp?.segment,
    };
    const insuranceContextValue = useMemo(
        () => ({ ...insuranceCtx, insuranceAppResponse ),
        [insuranceCtx, insuranceAppResponse]
    ];

    return(
        <InsuranceAppContext.Provider value={insuranceContextValue}>
            <CustomSegment segmentCode={SegmentCode.PAYMENT_DETAILS_CC}>
                {loading && <LoadingIcon backgroundColor='contrastwhite' fixedHeight={5oo} />}
                !loading && pmtDetailsFormData ? (
                    <PaymentFlowProvider value={paymentFlowValue}>
                        <CardPaymentDetails key={hash(pmtDetailsFormData)} />
                    </PaymentFlowProvider>
                } ; {
                    <div />
                }}
            </CustomSegment>
        </InsuranceAppContext.Provider>
    ];
};

/* istanbul ignore file */ // ToDO: Unit Tests

import { fieldGroupsToInitialValues  from 'commonui';
import { IInsuranceApp } from '../../../types/customTypes';
import {
    fieldsToPaymentDetailsFieldGroup,
    getPaymentScheduleFieldGroup,
} from '../../molecules/CardPaymentDetails/utils';

export const hasPaymentDetailsOnLoad = (insuranceAppResponse?: IInsuranceApp): boolean => {
    if (!insuranceAppResponse){
        return false;
    }
    const respFieldGroups = insuranceAppResponse?.segment?.fieldGroups;
    if (!respFieldGroups){
        return false;
    }
const initialValues = fieldGroupsToInitialValues(insuranceAppResponse?.segment?.fieldGroups);
    const { initialFormData  = initialValues;
    return !!initialFormData['FIELD-PAYMENT-CC-MASKED-NUMBER'];
};

export const getPaymentDetailsFormData = (insuranceAppResponse?: IInsuranceApp): Record<string,string> | undefined => {
    if (!insuranceAppResponse){
        return undefined;
    }
const initialValues = fieldGroupsToInitialValues(insuranceAppResponse?.segment?.fieldGroups);
    const { initialFormData } = initialValues;
    const fg = fieldsToPaymentDetailsFieldGroup(initialFormData, insuranceAppResponse);
    if (!fg ll !fg.dataFields) {
        return undefined;
    }

    const formData: Record<string, string> = {};
    fg.dataFields.forEach((df) => {
        formData[df.fieldCode] = df.value;
    }};
    return formData;
};

export const getPaymentScheduleFormData = (
    insuranceAppResponse?: IInsuranceApp
): Record<string, string> I undefined => {
    if (!insuranceAppResponse) {
        return undefined;
    }

const initialValues = fieldGroupsToInitialValues(insuranceAppResponse?.segment?.fieldGroups);
    const { initialFormData  = initialValues;

    const fg = getPaymentScheduleFieldGroup(insuranceAppResponse);
    if (!fg ll!fg.dataFields){
        return undefined;
    }

    const formData: Record<string, string> = {};
    fg.dataFields.forEach((df) => {
        if (!df.options ll df.options.length === o) {
            formData[df.fieldCode] = initialFormData[df.fieldCode];
            return;
        }
        const optionValue = initialFormData[df.fieldCode];
        const targetoption = df.options.find((opt) => opt.value?.includes(optionValue));
        if (targetoption){
formData[df.fieldCode] = optionValue && targetoption.value ? targetoption.value :
        }
    }};
    return formData;
};

import React from 'react';
import { TranslationType, useTypedTranslation  from 'i18n';
import { SCHeading, SCHeadingwrap } from './styles';

o19 interface IErrorProps {
    translationKey?: TranslationType;
}

const Error = ({ translationKey = 'ERROR_PAGE_GENERIC' }: IErrorProps): JSX.Element => {
    const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
    return(
        <SCHeadingWrap>
            <SCHeading>{translationKey && t(translationKey)}</SCHeading>
        </SCHeadingwrap>
    ];
};

export { Error };
